Allan-Herndon-Dudley syndrome associated with SLC16A2 c.1384G>A (p.Gly462Arg): A family case report with retrospective molecular diagnosis in a deceased sibling.推荐
PMID 42994898 · Medicine · 2026 Nov 06
作者：Li L, Li J, Li Z, Lv H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：RATIONALE: Allan-Herndon-Dudley syndrome (monocarboxylate transporter 8 deficiency) is an X-linked disorder caused by pathogenic variants in SLC16A2, with severe neurodevelopmental impairment and a characteristic high-triiodothyronine (T3)/low-thyroxine (T4) thyroid profile. SLC16A2 c.1384G> A (p.Gly462Arg; NM_006517.5) was reported previously. We describe a Chinese family with the same variant, including retrospective molecular confirmation in a deceased maternal half brother. PATIENT CONCERNS: The proband was evaluated at 4.3 years for profound developmental delay. He could not sit, stand, or walk independently and had an intelligence quotient of 36, axial hypotonia with peripheral hypertonia, dystonia, muscle atrophy, joint contractures, and bilateral cryptorchidism. DIAGNOSIS: Thyroid testing showed elevated free triiodothyronine and total triiodothyronine, decreased free thyroxine and total thyroxine, and mildly elevated thyroid-stimulating hormone. Brain magnetic resonance imaging showed delayed myelination, a thin corpus callosum, and widened frontal subarachnoid spaces. Whole-exome sequencing identified hemizygous SLC16A2 c.1384G> A (p.Gly462Arg); Sanger sequencing confirmed the variant in the proband and heterozygosity in his mother. The same hemizygous variant was detected in DNA from the archived neonatal dried blood spot of a maternal half brother who had severe developmental delay and died at 16 months. Thyroid tests, neuroimaging, and cause of death were not recorded for the deceased child. INTERVENTIONS: The proband received physical, occupational, and speech therapy and underwent bilateral orchiopexy. Triiodothyroacetic acid was not available at diagnosis. OUTCOMES: At the last documented follow-up at age 5 years, he remained unable to sit, stand, or walk independently. Clinical notes described improved head control, but no repeat standardized developmental score was available. Epileptiform electroencephalogram discharges persisted, but no clinical seizures were documented. LESSONS: This family provides additional clinical and segregation evidence for p.Gly462Arg. Testing an archived neonatal blood spot established the diagnosis in the deceased sibling and strengthened genetic counseling. SLC16A2 testing should be considered in boys with unexplained severe neurodevelopmental delay and a high-triiodothyronine/low-thyroxine pattern. Functional studies are still required to define the effect of p.Gly462Arg on monocarboxylate transporter 8 trafficking and thyroid hormone transport.
Vagus Nerve Stimulation as a Potential Therapeutic Strategy for Alcohol Use Disorder.推荐
PMID 42994768 · Alcohol (Fayetteville, N.Y.) · 2026 11 06
作者：Blinchevsky N, Weed M, Liangpunsakul S, Llorente C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Alcohol use disorder (AUD) contributes to approximately 3 million deaths worldwide each year and leads to substantial emotional, financial, and social implications for individuals and their families. AUD leads to multi-system damage, including the immune system, gut microbiome, neuroendocrine pathways, and the brain. Current treatments for AUD, focused primarily on behavioral and pharmaceutical interventions, insufficiently address alcohol's extensive impact on multiple body systems. Vagus nerve stimulation (VNS) has shown therapeutic benefits across a spectrum of neurological conditions, including epilepsy, depression, and addictive disorders. More recently, VNS is being investigated as a potential promising adjunctive treatment for AUD, which may complement currently used treatments by targeting many of the body systems dysregulated in AUD. Preclinical and clinical models have evaluated different VNS paradigms, including invasive, noninvasive, and minimally invasive approaches targeting motor and cognitive rehabilitation, withdrawal symptoms, and relapse during abstinence. However, while VNS has many promising potential benefits for improving AUD outcomes, direct evidence that VNS reduces alcohol consumption, alcohol seeking, or craving in AUD remains limited, and there are several logistical and ethical considerations that researchers, clinicians, and policymakers should evaluate before VNS is considered standard of care. Additionally, optimization of VNS parameters, modality (implanted versus noninvasive or minimally invasive), and development of responder biomarkers could better inform patient selection for VNS. Although significant work remains before transitioning VNS into clinical practice, VNS offers an exciting opportunity for adjunctive treatment in AUD that could potentially target multi-system AUD dysfunction.
Pilocarpine disrupts spontaneous rhythmic inhibitory bursting in the basolateral amygdala, promoting a transition to hyperexcitability.推荐
PMID 42994720 · Experimental neurology · 2026 11 06
作者：Aroniadou-Anderjaska V, Pidoplichko VI, Figueiredo TH, Braga MFM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：The view that seizures arise from an imbalance between excitatory and inhibitory activity, favoring excessive excitation, provides a valid but simplified description of the synaptic interactions underlying seizure initiation. Synchronous, rhythmic activity of inhibitory interneurons is known for imposing order in the activity of neuronal networks, often shaping or giving rise to local and long-range oscillations; disruption of rhythmic inhibitory activity could contribute to network hyperexcitability. In the rat basolateral amygdala (BLA)-a seizure-prone region that is also key to seizure initiation by muscarinic receptor hyperstimulation after organophosphate (OP) exposure-principal neurons display rhythmic, large-amplitude spontaneous IPSC (sIPSC) "bursts" at an average frequency of 0.5 Hz. Here, we used pilocarpine, a muscarinic agonist, to investigate whether disruption of rhythmic inhibitory activity contributes to the mechanisms by which muscarinic receptor hyperstimulation promotes BLA hyperexcitability. In voltage-clamp, pilocarpine suppressed the sIPSC bursts while increasing the background sIPSC activity and the sEPSC-to-sIPSC ratio, in principal neurons; in interneurons, pilocarpine suppressed the rhythmic sEPSC bursts while increasing background sEPSC activity. In current-clamp, pilocarpine produced depolarization and firing bursts in principal cells and sustained, high-frequency firing in interneurons when applied to normal medium or in the presence of CNQX, D-AP5, and bicuculline. Suppression of the sIPSC bursts by pilocarpine, in principal neurons, was mediated via M1 muscarinic receptors. Paraoxon, an OP, also disrupted the sIPSC bursts while increasing background sIPSCs. Thus, muscarinic receptor hyperstimulation in the BLA induces hyperexcitability by disrupting rhythmic inhibition while increasing firing of both principal neurons and interneurons.
When colours speak: context-dependent chromatic expression during predation in Sepia (acanthosepion) pharaonis.浏览
PMID 42994598 · Behavioural processes · 2026 11 06
作者：Schott O, Shashar N, Chiao CC, Dickel L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Predator-prey encounters constitute high-risk events for both interacting parties. These encounters are accompanied by changes in internal state, notably through increased arousal. Although cephalopods are renowned for their dynamic body patterns, the extent to which these chromatic displays track internal arousal or emotional valence during predation remains largely unresolved. We investigated seven chromatic components expressed by five subadults Sepia (acanthosepion) pharaonis (Ehrenberg, 1831) under controlled variations of prey accessibility, social context, and hunting outcomes to determine whether body-pattern dynamics reflect underlying emotion-like states. Chromatic expression intensified as predation progressed, peaking when prey was held in the arms. This rise was dominated by the Fragmented dark mantle component, which could serve as a potential marker of increased arousal. In contrast, one component - red fins, was selectively upregulated in situations with presumed negative valence, including inaccessible prey or the presence of a conspecific. Papillate skin expression decreased during prey capture, with papillae becoming less prominent at the moment of prey seizure, potentially contributing to improved hydrodynamic efficiency. Together, these findings provide the first evidence that S. pharaonis cuttlefish exhibits context-dependent, dissociable chromatic signatures during prey capture. These differences are consistent with the expression of arousal and valence. Our work offers a quantitative framework for interpreting cephalopod body patterns as indicators of internal states and informs broader theories on the evolution of emotional expression in non-human animals.
Single-Stage Resection and Customized PEEK Cranioplasty for Intracranial Tumors with Transosseous Involvement: A Single-Institution Case Series.推荐
PMID 42994525 · World neurosurgery · 2026 11 06
作者：Hafizka Y, Harary M, Salcedo-Sifuentes J, Shih R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: Single-stage cranioplasty following resection of intracranial neoplasms with transosseous involvement enables immediate cranial protection and may avoid delays to adjuvant therapy. While titanium mesh and polymethyl methacrylate have been widely used, they carry risks of long-term exposure, thermal conductivity, and imaging artifacts. Polyetheretherketone (PEEK) is a biocompatible alternative that allows anatomic reconstruction through computer-aided design and manufacturing (CAD/CAM). We report our institutional experience using patient-specific, prefabricated PEEK implants for synchronous reconstruction following tumor resection. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed patients who underwent single-stage resection of intracranial neoplasms and immediate cranioplasty with patient-specific PEEK implants by a single surgeon at the University of California, Los Angeles. Demographic, operative, and postoperative data were analyzed to assess feasibility and outcomes. RESULTS: Ten patients (70% female; mean age 55 ± 16 years) underwent single-stage reconstruction, most commonly for meningioma (80%). Median operative time was 336 minutes (IQR 245-454), and median blood loss 100 mL. All implants were successfully seated, with minor adjustment required in 3 cases. Median hospital stay was 2 days (IQR 2-3). Over a median follow-up of 22 months (IQR 6-28), no infections, implant exposures, or migrations occurred. Three patients (30%) developed postoperative seizures and three (30%) developed subcutaneous fluid collections, one of which was persistent. CONCLUSIONS: Single-stage patient-specific PEEK cranioplasty following oncologic resection was technically feasible in this small series, providing immediate cranial protection and restoration of cranial contour. Larger prospective studies are needed to define its complication profile, durability, and potential advantages for imaging surveillance and adjuvant therapy.
Phytocannabinoids in neurologic, neuropsychiatric, inflammatory, pain, cardiometabolic, hepatic, renal, and neoplastic disorders: Pharmacology, clinical evidence, and translational perspectives.推荐
PMID 42994510 · Pharmacology & therapeutics · 2026 11 06
作者：Trojnar E, Matyas C, Yokus B, Haskó G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Phytocannabinoids are pharmacologically diverse constituents of Cannabis sativa whose actions go beyond canonical cannabinoid receptors. Δ9-Tetrahydrocannabinol (THC) and cannabidiol (CBD) are best characterized, while cannabigerol, cannabidivarin, tetrahydrocannabivarin, cannabichromene, cannabinol, and related compounds are emerging candidates. This review integrates molecular pharmacology, pharmacokinetics, safety, and therapeutic evidence across neurologic, neuropsychiatric, inflammatory, pain, cardiovascular and metabolic, hepatic, renal, and neoplastic disorders. The strongest evidence supports pharmaceutical-grade oral CBD as adjunctive therapy for seizures associated with Dravet syndrome, Lennox-Gastaut syndrome, and tuberous sclerosis complex, and standardized THC-CBD formulations for selected patients with multiple sclerosis-associated spasticity. Emerging clinical signals in schizophrenia, pain, fibromyalgia, sleep disturbance, substance-use disorders, dermatologic conditions, sickle cell disease, and other symptom-focused indications support continued development. However, efficacy remains compound-, formulation-, exposure-, and indication-specific. In oncology, supportive-care applications must remain distinct from tumor-directed treatment, for which clinical efficacy has not been established. Preclinical studies show coherent effects on neuronal excitability, glial and immune activation, endothelial dysfunction, oxidative and nitrative stress, mitochondrial injury, fibrosis, and regulated cell death. These findings support further investigation in neurodegeneration, ischemia-reperfusion injury, diabetic cardiovascular disease, immune-mediated disorders, hepatic and renal injury, and selected cancers. Translation is constrained by product heterogeneity, variable exposure, uncertain target engagement, model differences, drug interactions, and underpowered trials. Standardized products, exposure-informed dosing, pharmacodynamic biomarkers, responder-enriched designs, and indication-specific randomized studies can address these barriers. Phytocannabinoids therefore represent a promising therapeutic platform whose potential will be best realized by matching defined compounds and formulations to biologically coherent diseases, mechanisms, treatment windows, and patient populations.
Validation of the Scottish epilepsy deaths study (SEDS) score using routinely collected data in Wales: a retrospective cohort study.推荐
PMID 42994382 · Seizure · 2026 10 29
作者：Tripathi S, Daniels H, Lacey AS, Mbizvo GK et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: The Scottish Epilepsy Deaths Study (SEDS) Score was developed to identify individuals at increased risk of epilepsy-related death. We externally validated the SEDS Score within the Welsh Population using the Secure Anonymised Information Linkage (SAIL) databank. METHODS: This is a retrospective cohort study of adults (≥18 years) with epilepsy living in Wales, 2015-2019. Five annual cohorts were created, with individuals followed for one year. Epilepsy-related deaths were defined as deaths with epilepsy recorded on death certificates. SEDS Scores were calculated for all individuals at the beginning of each cohort year and used to categorise low, medium, high, and very-high risk groups based on individual SEDS Scores. We used logistic regression to compare odds-ratios (ORs) for epilepsy-related death in each of the SEDS Score risk groups. RESULTS: We found 423 epilepsy-related deaths amongst 117,724 epilepsy person-years. An increasing SEDS Score was associated with increasing risk of epilepsy-related death. Odds ratios (95% confidence intervals) for epilepsy-related deaths within one year (compared to the reference category (SEDS Score = 0)) for low, medium, high and very high-risk SEDS Score categories were 4.5 (1.6-10.0); 1.3 (1.0-1.7); 3.2 (2.4-4.3); and 7.6 (5.6-10.4) respectively. CONCLUSION: The SEDS Score demonstrates predictive value in identifying individuals at higher risk of epilepsy-related death within the Welsh population. SEDS Score performed particularly well for the high and very high-risk categories and may help prioritise targeted preventative interventions.
Polystyrene nanoplastic exposure exacerbates epileptic vulnerability in SCN1A mutation models.推荐
PMID 42994359 · Journal of hazardous materials · 2026 11 03
作者：Jo JH, Park SB, Jung WH, Lee J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Micro- and nanoplastics are pervasive contaminants detected in human tissues that cross the blood-brain barrier to induce neuroinflammation, apoptosis, and neurotoxicity. Here, we examined whether polystyrene nanoplastic (PS-NP) exposure alters seizure susceptibility in SCN1A+/- cerebral organoids and Scn1a+/- mice, modeling the severe genetic epilepsy Dravet syndrome. At equal mass concentrations, 50 nm PS-NPs penetrated organoids more deeply and upregulated inflammatory and neurotoxic gene expression more robustly than 100 nm particles, inducing apoptotic activation and significant DNA fragmentation. Exposure disrupted steroid homeostasis-causing genotype-dependent cortisol elevations-and perturbed neurotransmitter systems in SCN1A-deficient models via reduced GABA and glutamate, accompanied by decreased GAD2 and increased VGLUT2 expression. In vivo, exposed Scn1a+/- mice exhibited hyperactivity and reached Racine stages 3-5 more rapidly under thermal induction, demonstrating a reduced seizure threshold. Pharmacological inhibition of 11β-hydroxysteroid dehydrogenase type 1 reversed this hyperactivity but failed to restore the seizure threshold. Together, these findings indicate that PS-NP exposure exacerbates seizure vulnerability in genetically susceptible backgrounds, accompanied by marked neurochemical, endocrine, and apoptotic disruptions. These results support a 'second-hit' model wherein environmental nanoplastics act as external triggers in genetic epilepsy, underscoring the vital need to incorporate gene-environment interactions into neurotoxicological risk assessments.
Specific learning disorders in children with epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42994138 · Brain & development · 2026 11 06
作者：Kaga Y
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：It is estimated that between half and approximately 70% of children with epilepsy experience learning difficulties, making this a prominent issue during the school-age years. Possible causes include mild intellectual disability, comorbid neurodevelopmental disorders, and sleep disorders; among these, cases with comorbid specific learning disorder (SLD) are by no means rare. In particular, regarding developmental dyslexia, which is the most common condition, it has been reported to be frequent in conditions associated with the left temporal lobe, such as self-limited epilepsy with centrotemporal spikes (SeLECTS), temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE), and periventricular heterotopia (PNH). Reports of developmental dysgraphia have also been noted in TLE and SeLECTS. Overall, it has been reported that children with epilepsy tend to have lower math skills. It is known that the severity is higher in idiopathic generalized epilepsy than in focal epilepsy, and it is speculated that this involves networks such as the parietal lobe and impairments in working memory. Since epilepsy can cause SLD symptoms due to various anatomical abnormalities and the development of electrical networks, it is important to consider SLD as one of the causes of learning difficulties when providing clinical care for epilepsy.
Real-world efficacy and safety profile of sulthiame treatment in drug-resistant childhood epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42994001 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 03
作者：Yeniay Süt N, Yıldırım M, Tabanlı FP, Bektaş Ö et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Sulthiame (STM) is a potent carbonic anhydrase inhibitor primarily used in focal epilepsy syndromes; however, clinical data regarding its efficacy and safety in broader drug-resistant childhood epilepsy remain limited. This study aimed to evaluate the clinical efficacy, electroencephalographic response, and safety profile of STM in a cohort of pediatric patients with drug-resistant epilepsy. METHODS: A retrospective medical chart review was conducted for patients under 18 years of age treated with STM at a tertiary pediatric neurology clinic between 2011 and 2024. Data including demographic characteristics, etiology, seizure semiology, epilepsy syndromes, treatment duration, and dosage were analyzed. Electroencephalographic (EEG) findings were assessed using EEG recordings obtained within 1-3 months after initiation of sulthiame therapy. Seizure frequency outcomes were assessed by comparing baseline seizure frequency with seizure frequency at the final available clinical follow-up. RESULTS: Sixty-one patients (55.7% female; median seizure onset: 14 months) were included. A structural etiology was identified in 50.8% of patients, and 62.3% had a well-defined epilepsy syndrome. At the final available clinical follow-up, 41% (n = 25) of patients achieved complete seizure freedom, while 16.4% (n = 10) showed a ≥ 50% reduction in seizure frequency. Conversely, 42.6% demonstrated a < 50% reduction. A higher number of previously failed anti-seizure medications (ASMs) was significantly associated with a poorer treatment response (p = 0.014). EEG improvement was observed in 44.3% of the cohort. Notably, focal seizure semiology was associated with favorable EEG outcomes, whereas combined semiology was linked to electroencephalographic worsening (p = 0.026). STM was generally well-tolerated; adverse effects were reported in only nine patients, with treatment discontinuation required in only two cases (3.2%). CONCLUSIONS: Sulthiame is an effective and well-tolerated therapeutic option for children with drug-resistant epilepsy, particularly those with focal semiology. Our findings suggest that a higher burden of prior ASM treatment negatively impacts response rates, highlighting the potential benefit of considering STM earlier in the treatment algorithm for specific pediatric populations.
Socioeconomic deprivation and spatial inequalities in epilepsy-related mortality in Peru: A national ecological study, 2017-2023.推荐
PMID 42994000 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 05
作者：Guevara-Tirado A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Epilepsy-related mortality may reflect socioeconomic and territorial inequalities, as well as disparities in access to healthcare. Subnational evidence from Latin America remains limited. OBJECTIVE: To evaluate socioeconomic and spatial inequalities in epilepsy-related mortality across provinces in Peru. METHODS: A national ecological study using SINADEF data from 2017 to 2023 was conducted. Epilepsy-related mortality was defined as any mention of ICD-10 codes G40-G41. Provincial deprivation combined poverty, rurality, and low educational attainment using principal component analysis. Official annual provincial population denominators, age-standardized rates, the slope index of inequality (SII), relative index of inequality (RII), negative binomial regression, and a Bayesian BYM2 model were used. Code-specific negative-binomial sensitivity analyses evaluated G40 and G41 separately. RESULTS: A total of 4547 deaths across 196 provinces were analyzed. The age-standardized mortality rate was 1.99 per 100,000 person-years and increased from 1.73 in the least deprived quintile to 2.98 in the most deprived quintile (Q5/Q1 =1.73). The RII was 1.81 (95% CI 1.34-2.45), and the SII was + 1.27 per 100,000 person-years (95% CI 0.64-1.91). In the BYM2 model, each 1-SD increase in deprivation was associated with 18% higher mortality (RR 1.18; 95% CrI 1.10-1.27; P[RR>1]=1.00). G40 was mentioned in 4196 deaths and G41 in 507; 156 certificates contained both. In code-specific sensitivity models, the deprivation association persisted for G40 (RR 1.19; 95% CI 1.11-1.27) and G41 (RR 1.34; 95% CI 1.20-1.49). The socioeconomic gradient persisted over time. CONCLUSIONS: Epilepsy-related mortality showed a persistent socioeconomic and spatial gradient. Rurality was the most robust individual component, supporting the evaluation of territorial barriers to healthcare.
MRgFUS for drug-resistant epilepsy: Preliminary report for prospective clinical trial.推荐
PMID 42993999 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 01
作者：Su YH, Chen YH, Shih YC, Chou CC et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To assess the safety, feasibility, and preliminary clinical outcomes of magnetic resonance-guided focused ultrasound (MRgFUS) for drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). METHODS: This TFDA-regulated, open-label study enrolled four adults with DRE. MRgFUS targeted the anterior nucleus of the thalamus (ANT), the centromedian nucleus (CM), the posterior corpus callosum (CC), or the hypothalamic hamartoma (HH). The primary endpoints were safety and feasibility, while secondary endpoints included seizure frequency, EEG results, and postoperative MRI findings. RESULTS: The mean age of the patients was 34.7 years and the mean SDR was 0.38. Two of the procedures were performed under local anesthesia and three under general anesthesia. No serious adverse events, permanent neurological deficit, or postoperative cognitive decline were observed. At follow-up ranging from 3 to 15 months, seizure outcomes varied. The CM-treated patient showed stable overall seizure frequency with a 42.6% reduction in focal seizures. Posterior CC lesioning reduced interhemispheric EEG synchrony and was associated with a 15.9% reduction in generalized seizures. The HH case displayed a decrease in epileptiform activity on EEG and a 55.5% reduction of gelastic seizures, despite the absence of a definitive lesion. In the ANT case, the initial procedure was discontinued prior to reaching therapeutic temperatures, and the repeat treatment under general anesthesia was associated with a 66.0% seizure reduction at final follow-up. CONCLUSIONS: MRgFUS achieved lesion formation at selected epilepsy-related targets, including the first reported applications to the posterior CC and CM for epilepsy. However, the ability to achieve therapeutic temperatures and produce a definitive lesion varied among patients and was influenced by skull characteristics of the anatomical target. Given the small, heterogeneous cohort and limited follow-up, the observed clinical outcomes should be considered preliminary. Larger studies with longer follow-up are needed to establish efficacy and optimize treatment parameters.
Brivaracetam for the termination of status epilepticus: A systematic review and meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 42993998 · Epilepsy research · 2026 10 31
作者：Shelbaya AN, Ali M, Elsayed D, AbouShawareb H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Status epilepticus (SE) is an emergency neurological disorder which may lead to irreversible brain injury and death if not dealt with appropriately. About 30-50% of patients develop refractory SE due to failure to respond to the current antiseizure medications (ASMs). Recently, brivaracetam has been evaluated in studies to be implemented in management protocols. It showed promising efficacy similar to that of levetiracetam but better tolerability in SE patients in some studies, whereas it was less effective in other studies. OBJECTIVE: To collect the available evidence about brivaracetam's efficacy and assess its possibility to be considered in the second-line medications in SE management protocol. METHODS: A systematic review and meta-analysis were carried out following PRISMA and Cochrane Collaboration standards. Studies evaluating brivaracetam in SE patients were searched in PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus till March 2026. Quality assessment was examined using Newcastle-Ottawa scale (NOS). Statistical analysis was conducted using a random-effects model in R software. The response rate outcome was pooled and expressed as event rates with 95% confidence intervals, and heterogeneity was calculated using I² and chi2 p statistics. RESULTS: Ten studies were analyzed. The studies were heterogeneous in the number of previous medications before brivaracetam, the period between the onset of status epilepticus and brivaracetam administration, and the loading and maintenance doses of brivaracetam. The overall response rate was 0.52 (95% CI 0.45-0.59, p = 0.62), with moderate heterogeneity across studies (I2 = 41.61%, τ = 0.29, p = 0.045). CONCLUSION: Brivaracetam could be an effective second-line drug with roughly half of patients achieving remission of status epilepticus. BRV administration within < 24 h with a dose range of 200-300 mg has been associated with treatment response. High-quality comparative RCTs are required to rank it within the current ASMs and define its place in SE protocols.
Imaging fast neural circuit activity in brain and nerve with Electrical Impedance Tomography (fnEIT): review, method and engineering aspects.推荐
PMID 42993982 · Journal of neural engineering · 2026 11 06
作者：Mason K, Aristovich KY, Holder DS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Fast neural electrical impedance tomography (fnEIT) is a technique for imaging neural activity in brains or nerves, with a resolution of milliseconds and millimetres, using an applied current and boundary or depth voltage measurement. Proof of principle has been demonstrated in animal studies, but it is yet to be adopted into common use. Objective The purpose of this review is to provide an introduction and overview of the method and technical aspects of fnEIT research and its place in the biomedical sciences. Topics covered include bioimpedance of action potentials, fnEIT studies to date, similar EIT methods and technical innovations. Approach A critical review of the fnEIT literature has been performed. Promising areas of future work and their applications are highlighted, including challenges that must be overcome. Main Results It is shown that the fields of nerve imaging and neuromodulation, cognitive neuroscience in animals and humans, and epileptic seizure localisation could benefit from the ability of fnEIT to image millisecond scale neuronal depolarization throughout brain or nerve. This is achievable with existing methods but with current limitations of the need for implanted cranial or nerve electrodes and averaging to a repeated stimulus. Current approaches to overcome this include CMOS integrated active electrodes, frequency division multiplexing for parallel signal acquisition, and recording with atomic magnetometers. With these, it is a realistic possibility to achieve non-invasive imaging of fast neural activity in real-time with an electrode-atomic magnetometer helmet in human subjects with a resolution of mm and msec. Significance. This review serves as a general guide for those working in related fields or those considering the use of EIT. With the above advances, it is a realistic possibility to achieve non-invasive imaging of fast neural activity in real-time with an electrode-atomic magnetometer helmet in human subjects with a resolution of mm and msec.
Evaluation of Volumetric Absorptive Microsampling as an Alternative Matrix for Therapeutic Drug Monitoring of Antiseizure Medications: A Robust Clinical Validation Study.推荐
PMID 42993815 · Therapeutic drug monitoring · 2026 11 06
作者：Cobo-Golpe M, Lendoiro E, Blanco-Ces M, Melcón C et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Microsampling techniques, such as volumetric absorptive microsampling (VAMS), are gaining popularity for therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) of various drugs. VAMS is especially useful for conditions that affect more vulnerable patients, such as children and older patients. Epilepsy is a chronic disease that is treated with antiseizure medications (ASMs) and must be monitored throughout the patient's lifetime. However, before their use in clinical settings, clinical validation must be performed to assess their suitability and determine the correlation between VAMS concentrations and plasma concentrations for which therapeutic ranges are established. METHODS: Paired VAMS, plasma, and whole blood (WB) samples were collected and analyzed from 159 patients treated with at least 1 of the 8 ASMs studied. Clinical validation was performed for levetiracetam (LEV) and valproic acid (VPA) with >60 positive samples, and preliminary clinical validation was performed for lamotrigine (LTG) with >30 samples using Passing-Bablok and Bland-Altman analyses. RESULTS: For all 3 ASMs, concentrations in VAMS correlated well with both plasma and WB concentrations (Pearson ρ > 0.98). The analytical requirements established by the European Medicines Agency and Food and Drug Administration were met when comparing VAMS and plasma concentrations. However, in some cases, a correction of the concentrations was required for comparison with WB. CONCLUSIONS: The results demonstrate a good equivalence of VAMS and plasma concentrations. This renders VAMS a suitable sample for the TDM of LEV, VPA, and possibly LTG.
Motor Cortex Stimulation in the Treatment of Chronic Neuropathic Pain: Real-World Data on Long-Term Outcomes, Predictors of Response, and Safety.推荐
PMID 42993119 · Neuromodulation : journal of the International Neuromodulation Society · 2026 10 05
作者：Isagulyan E, Dorokhov E, Kondratyev V, Tomskiy A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: Motor cortex stimulation (MCS) has been used for chronic neuropathic pain for over 35 years, yet long-term efficacy and safety data remain extremely limited. This study aimed to present real-world data on the long-term outcomes, predictors of response, and safety of MCS in clinical practice. MATERIALS AND METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted on 20 patients (median age 52.5 years) who underwent epidural MCS implantation at a single neurosurgical center between 2004 and 2014. Indications included neuropathic pain lasting more than three months with an intensity >5 on the Numeric Rating Scale (NRS) refractory to conservative therapy. Preoperative transcranial magnetic stimulation (TMS) and functional magnetic resonance imaging with neuronavigation were used for electrode targeting. Pain intensity was assessed using a questionnaire based on the Brief Pain Inventory. Stimulation efficacy was defined as ≥50% pain reduction at the last follow-up. Statistical analysis employed nonparametric methods (Wilcoxon signed-rank test). The median follow-up was 11 years. RESULTS: At last follow-up, median pain intensity decreased from 8 to 4 points on the NRS (p < 0.05). Long-term pain reduction ≥50% was achieved in 14 of 19 patients (73.7%). One patient was excluded from the long-term efficacy analysis due to system explantation for infection three months postoperatively. Two patients (10%) experienced a loss of efficacy over time. TMS demonstrated a positive predictive value of 71.43% but a negative predictive value of only 33.33% in predicting MCS efficacy. Infectious complications leading to system explantation occurred in two patients (10%). No seizures, hematomas, or permanent neurologic deficits were observed. CONCLUSIONS: MCS provides clinically significant long-term pain reduction in 70% of patients with chronic neuropathic pain, alongside subjectively improved quality of life and reduced analgesic requirements. A single ineffective TMS session should not preclude MCS implantation because the negative predictive value is low. Larger randomized controlled trials are warranted to verify these findings.
Medication Administration Errors by Caregivers of Children with Chronic Illness in Home Settings: A Systematic Review.浏览
PMID 42993078 · International journal for quality in health care : journal of the International Society for Quality in Health Care · 2026 11 06
作者：Zakaria AS, Otchi EH, Chukwuere PC, Tenza IS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Home-based medication administration is vital for managing paediatric chronic conditions but is prone to errors that affect patient safety and outcomes. Children suffering from​ chronic conditions such as cancer, diabetes, epilepsy, and sickle cell disease frequently require medication outside hospital settings, necessitating non-professional caregivers to administer and oversee these medications. This systematic review synthesised global evidence on medication administration errors (MAEs) by caregivers in home settings. METHODS: In accordance with the JBI methodology and PRISMA 2020 guidelines, a search was conducted in PubMed, Web of Science, ScienceDirect, and Scopus for English-language studies published between January 2015 and December 2025. The methodological quality of the studies was evaluated using the JBI critical appraisal checklists, and data were collected using standardized JBI forms and were synthesized narratively. RESULTS: Out of 25,508 records reviewed, 12 studies met the inclusion criteria. The prevalence of errors, as observed directly, ranged from 30% to 80%, with pharmacist-led assessments identifying a high rate of 89.89%. Dosing errors were the most frequently reported, appearing in six of the 12 studies, followed by errors related to timing and the method or route of administration. Clinical harm was reported in 8-12.8% of the error cases, resulting in preventable hospitalizations and treatment failures due to chronic underdosing. The most consistently noted risk factors were polypharmacy, insufficient health literacy among caregivers, and limited caregiver proficiency in English. CONCLUSIONS: Medication errors at home are frequent and have significant clinical implications for children with chronic illnesses. To address this, healthcare systems should incorporate structured education for caregivers into discharge plans and enhance access to dosing aids and digital tools, especially in resource-constrained areas such as sub-Saharan Africa.
Global Age Shift and Growing Inequities in the Idiopathic Epilepsy Burden among Children and Adolescents (0-19 Years), 1990-2050.推荐
PMID 42993073 · Neuroepidemiology · 2026 10 15
作者：Wu Y, Niu Y, Jiao X, Xu Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Idiopathic epilepsy (IE) remains a leading cause of neurological disability in the pediatric and adolescent population. While global mortality has declined, the evolving structural distribution of the disease burden and the potential impact of external shocks like the COVID-19 pandemic remain poorly quantified. We aimed to assess the global, regional, and national burden of IE among individuals aged 0-19 years from 1990 to 2021 and project trajectories through 2050. METHODS: Using data from the Global Burden of Disease (GBD) Study 2021, we analyzed incidence, prevalence, deaths, and disability-adjusted life years (DALYs) for IE across 204 countries and territories. Temporal trends were evaluated using joinpoint regression to estimate average annual percent changes (AAPCs). We employed bivariate spatial analysis to intersect IE burden with COVID-19 intensity and quantified health inequalities using the Slope Index of Inequality (SII) and Concentration Index. Future projections were generated via Bayesian age-period-cohort (BAPC) models. RESULTS: In 2021, the global age-standardized prevalence of IE reached 321.41 per 100,000, representing an 8.03% increase since 1990 (AAPC = 0.25%; 95% CI: 0.07%-0.43%). While age-standardized death rates decreased by 21.67% (AAPC = -0.79%; 95% CI: -0.99% to -0.58%) and DALY rates fell by 15.57% (AAPC = -0.54%; 95% CI: -0.72% to -0.37%), global incidence rose slightly (AAPC = 0.30%; 95% CI: 0.12%-0.47%). A shift in the demographic distribution of the disease burden was observed: the primary burden migrated from the <5 age group in 1990 to the 15-19 age group in 2021, likely driven by declining early childhood mortality. Bivariate spatial analysis identified 29 countries exhibiting geographic overlap between high baseline IE endemicity and intense COVID-19 impact. Inequality indices (SII and Concentration Index) confirmed a progressive consolidation of health loss within low socio-demographic index (SDI) regions. By 2050, despite projected declines in rates, absolute DALY counts are expected to remain high in low-resource settings due to demographic momentum. CONCLUSIONS: The global IE landscape among the 0-19-year population has transitioned into a "post-mortality" phase, characterized by improved survival but expanding chronic disability, particularly among late adolescents (15-19 years). The disproportionate burden in low SDI regions and the systemic vulnerabilities highlighted during the COVID-19 pandemic necessitate a paradigm shift from acute intervention to longitudinal, age-specific care and the urgent closure of the global "treatment gap."
Artificial Intelligence in Neurology: A Critical Narrative Review of Clinical Evidence, Translation, and Responsible Implementation.浏览
PMID 42992946 · Brain and behavior · 2026 Nov
作者：Zahran A, Fuqha R, Milhem A, Hajjeh O et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：PURPOSE: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly applied to neuroimaging, electrophysiology, clinical text, and digitally measured behavior, but reported performance often exceeds the maturity of clinical evidence. METHOD: We conducted a structured narrative review, informed by SANRA principles, of English-language human studies, reviews, and regulatory sources identified in PubMed/MEDLINE and IEEE Xplore from January 2022 through July 2026; older landmark studies were retained when necessary for historical or methodological context. We applied a common translational-evidence framework across six prespecified neurological domains to distinguish technical performance from external validation, prospective clinical evaluation, implementation, and demonstrated patient benefit. Evidence was appraised according to cohort representativeness, patient-level data separation, external and prospective validation, calibration, clinical comparators, regulatory status, workflow integration, and patient-level benefit. FINDING: Across stroke, dementia, Parkinson disease, epilepsy, multiple sclerosis, and neuro-oncology, the strongest evidence concerns narrowly defined imaging and signal-processing tasks. A cluster-randomized stroke study demonstrated shorter thrombectomy workflow time, and several externally validated models show improved portability. In contrast, many high-accuracy classification and segmentation studies remain retrospective, single-dataset, internally validated, and uncalibrated. Multimodal learning, foundation models, federated learning, and large language models broaden capability but introduce additional risks related to dataset shift, bias, opacity, privacy, and automation. CONCLUSION: Neuro-AI should be judged by its intended clinical task and evidence maturity rather than headline accuracy. Multicenter external validation, prospective workflow evaluation, calibration, subgroup reporting, human-factors testing, and post-deployment surveillance are prerequisites for safe and equitable clinical use.
Resting-State Functional Metrics Associated with Seizure Recurrence After Antiseizure Medication Withdrawal.推荐
PMID 42992711 · Journal of visualized experiments : JoVE · 2026 11 05
作者：Zhang Y, Liu Y, Gu X, Ma H et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Antiseizure medication (ASM) withdrawal after sustained seizure freedom requires a balanced estimate of recurrence risk, yet objective imaging correlates remain uncertain. This prospective observational protocol compared prewithdrawal resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) in 30 patients with epilepsy and 22 healthy controls (HC). All patients completed 12 months of follow-up after complete withdrawal; 16 developed recurrence (RG) and 14 remained seizure-free (NRG). Seizure-onset zones were temporal (n = 13), frontal (n = 9), generalized (n = 4), parietal/occipital (n = 2), and undetermined focal (n = 2). Metric-specific branches generated amplitude of low-frequency fluctuation (ALFF), fractional ALFF (fALFF), and regional homogeneity (ReHo) maps. Functional connectivity and graph metrics used the automated anatomical labeling atlas; network-based statistic (NBS) testing used 5,000 permutations. Models included group, age, and sex, with Holm correction for pairwise contrasts. RG showed higher left gyrus rectus fALFF than HC and NRG and higher left orbital frontal fALFF and right supplementary motor area ReHo than HC. NRG showed higher middle cingulate ALFF than HC, although this finding was less stable in sensitivity analyses. No global or nodal network metric differed among groups, and no functional connection survived NBS family-wise correction. These results associate seizure recurrence with localized prewithdrawal functional differences while providing no evidence for a corrected large-scale connectivity signature. The findings support regional hypothesis generation but do not establish an individual prediction tool.​.
SCN1A (C>T) rs3812718 Polymorphism and Phenytoin-Induced Adverse Drug Reactions: Genotyping Versus Therapeutic Drug Monitoring in a Prospective Cohort.浏览
PMID 42992702 · Therapeutic drug monitoring · 2026 11 06
作者：Kumar B, Natarajan A, Nair PP
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Phenytoin has a narrow therapeutic index, and therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) is routinely used to guide dosing. The SCN1A (C>T) rs3812718 polymorphism has been proposed as a pharmacogenomic marker of phenytoin response; however, whether genotyping provides additional predictive value once TDM is already available remains unclear. METHODS: A prospective cohort of 168 adult patients with epilepsy receiving phenytoin monotherapy was recruited from the neurology outpatient department of a tertiary care center. Participants were genotyped for SCN1A (C>T) rs3812718 using real-time polymerase chain reaction and followed for 6 months to assess adverse drug reactions (ADRs). Trough plasma phenytoin concentrations were measured and categorized as subtherapeutic, therapeutic, or supratherapeutic. Associations between genotype and ADRs were assessed overall, by TDM category, and using Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH) analysis stratified by TDM category. RESULTS: ADRs occurred in 155 of 168 participants (92.3%), predominantly involving the central nervous system (85.7%). The mutant genotype (CT/TT) was present in 81.5% of participants. No significant association was observed between SCN1A genotype and overall or system-specific ADRs (dominant model, P = 0.655; codominant model, P = 0.576). Genotype was also not associated with TDM category (dominant model, P = 0.263; codominant model, P = 0.583). CMH analysis stratified by TDM category showed no association (odds ratio, 0.37; 95% confidence interval, 0.05-2.72; P = 0.330). CONCLUSIONS: In this cohort, SCN1A (C>T) rs3812718 genotyping did not provide additional value beyond routine TDM in predicting phenytoin-induced ADRs. Phenytoin therapy may therefore continue to be guided by TDM rather than by routine genotyping for this variant.
Gene therapy for epilepsy: From SCN1A to broadened therapeutic horizons (Review).推荐
PMID 42992560 · Molecular medicine reports · 2026 11 06
作者：Qin R, Hu X, Jiang W, Wang J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Genetic epilepsy, for which the molecular mechanisms are relatively well defined in numerous monogenic forms, represents an attractive model for the transition from symptomatic seizure control to mechanism‑driven correction. However, gene therapy strategies differ in their mechanisms of action, adaptability to specific mutations and translational maturity, making rational selection of interventions based on variant‑specific functional consequences an important clinical challenge. The present review examined five nucleic acid‑based therapeutic strategies, namely gene supplementation, enhancement of endogenous gene expression, gene silencing, DNA editing and RNA editing, for major causative genes, using mutation‑specific functional consequences [loss‑of‑function (LOF) vs. gain‑of‑function] as the primary framework for therapeutic decision‑making. The mechanisms and suitability of each approach are analysed, and their performance in terms of cell‑type specificity, delivery vectors and safety is evaluated by integrating evidence from animal models, induced pluripotent stem cell‑derived neurons and organoids and clinical trials. The present review also summarised key translational barriers, including vector cargo constraints, blood‑brain barrier penetration, off‑target and immune‑associated risks, therapeutic dosing windows and the timing of developmental intervention. Antisense oligonucleotides and regulatory adeno‑associated viral approaches, supported by mature delivery platforms and manageable safety profiles, have progressed furthest towards clinical validation. Endogenous gene activation strategies, such as CRISPR activation, offer a gene replacement‑free approach for LOF disorders but remain preclinical. DNA and RNA editing, while potentially capable of durable disease modification, require further evidence on delivery efficiency and long‑term homeostatic safety. Gene therapy for genetic epilepsy is undergoing a key transition from proof‑of‑concept studies to individualised clinical application. Future progress will depend on integrating personalised intervention design, multi‑target therapeutic combinations, artificial intelligence‑assisted sequence optimisation and standardised translational models, with the potential to drive a paradigm shift from seizure suppression to mechanistic correction.
Epitope-dependent acute inhibition and potentiation of N-methyl-d-aspartate receptors by GluN1 autoantibodies.浏览
PMID 42992456 · The Journal of physiology · 2026 11 06
作者：Yang S, Heckmann J, Zettner M, Hasegawa K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Transfer of autoantibodies targeting ionotropic N-methyl-d-aspartate (NMDA) receptors from patients with autoimmune encephalitis to mice induces characteristic disease signs. The long-term effects of pathogenic antibodies include immunoglobulin G (IgG)-induced cross-linking and receptor internalization. We investigated the direct and immediate impact of two specific pathogenic patient-derived monoclonal autoantibodies (IgG #003-102 and #007-168) on NMDA receptor (NMDAR) function. We performed cell-attached recordings from cells transfected with the GluN1-3a and GluN2A subunits of NMDARs. IgG #003-102 binds to the R2 lobe of the amino-terminal domain (ATD) of the glycine-binding GluN1 subunit. It significantly reduced the number of simultaneous NMDAR openings compared to controls at both low and high concentrations of glutamate and glycine. Closer examination revealed that IgG #003-102 rapidly reduced the number of open receptors and shortened receptor-opening bursts. However, antigen-binding fragments of IgG #003-102 did not reduce receptor openings. By contrast, IgG #007-168, which binds to the top of the R1 lobe of the GluN1-ATD, potentiated the NMDAR response. In conclusion, patient-derived IgG #003-102 inhibits NMDARs within seconds, before receptor internalization, and the full IgG is required for this acute inhibitory effect. This suggests that monovalent IgG #003-102 constructs could be a new treatment strategy to shield a pathogenic epitope on NMDARs. KEY POINTS: N-methyl-d-aspartate (NMDA) receptors (NMDARs) are ionotropic glutamate receptors central to brain development and function. Anti-NMDAR encephalitis is the most common cause of non-viral autoimmune brain inflammation and is characterized by psychosis, dyskinesias, autonomic dysfunction and seizures. Symptoms are considered to result from autoantibodies that internalize surface NMDARs on neurons. Using patch clamp recordings, we tested the acute effects of two monoclonal anti-GluN1 autoantibodies and the Fab fragment of one of them on NMDARs. The autoantibody (IgG #003-102), which binds to the R2 lobe of the GluN1-amino-terminal domain (ATD), inhibits receptors within seconds. The entire IgG is required for this inhibition. By contrast, IgG #007-168, which binds to the top of the R1 lobe of the GluN1-ATD, potentiates receptor responses. By capitalizing on the null phenotype of a Fab from IgG #003-102, we propose monovalent constructs as a potential new treatment strategy to prevent pathogenic antibodies from causing receptor dysfunction.

Population-specific differences in ClinVar pathogenic and likely pathogenic variants of epilepsy-associated genes between East Asian and non-finnish European populations: a nucleotide-resolved gnomAD v4.1.1 analysis.推荐
PMID 42991897 · Frontiers in genetics · 2026 10 23
作者：Lai JX, He MF, Fan ZC, Yan FJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Pathogenic and likely pathogenic (P/LP) variants in ClinVar guide variant interpretation in clinical genetic testing, yet their population-specific frequency differences have not been systematically examined in epilepsy. We compiled 199 epilepsy-associated genes, extracted 46,746 unique P/LP variants from ClinVar, and retrieved allele frequencies from gnomAD v4.1.1 gene-level tables using nucleotide-resolved matching. Fisher's exact test with Benjamini-Hochberg correction (q < 0.05) identified nine variants significantly enriched in East Asian (EAS) relative to non-Finnish European (NFE) populations, with odds ratios of 36.3-867.9. Eight additional low-frequency EAS signals met the same enrichment criterion with at least two EAS alleles and an uncorrected p < 0.01 and are reported as exploratory. Thirteen of the seventeen variants reside in seven autosomal recessive genes, namely, ALDH7A1, ST3GAL5, GOSR2, AARS1, BRAT1, WWOX, and UBA5. In parallel, an rsID-level workflow, the standard approach in database mining, yielded 60 candidate EAS-enriched variants. Nucleotide-resolved matching confirmed only fifteen of these and additionally recovered two signals the rsID workflow missed, a false-positive rate of 75.0%. These findings show that epilepsy P/LP variant frequencies differ substantially between EAS and NFE populations and concentrate in recessive genes with direct relevance to carrier screening. rsID-level ClinVar-gnomAD integration systematically overestimates population specificity, and nucleotide-resolved allele matching should become a standard step in population frequency analyses.
Rapidly progressive cognitive decline and drug-induced encephalopathy secondary to teprotumumab for thyroid eye disease: a case report and narrative literature review.推荐
PMID 42991816 · Frontiers in immunology · 2026 10 22
作者：Cheng ZJ, Zhou SX, He ZY, Deng ZF et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Teprotumumab, a fully human monoclonal antibody targeting insulin-like growth factor 1 receptor (IGF-1R), is an approved targeted therapy for thyroid eye disease (TED). Teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy is an extremely rare but potentially severe central nervous system adverse event, with only three cases reported globally to date. We report the first Han-Chinese patient with Graves' disease and associated TED complicated by teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy, together with a narrative review of all published cases, to raise clinicians' awareness and improve early recognition and management of this rare complication. CASE PRESENTATION: A 56-year-old Chinese man with Graves' disease and TED developed acute progressive apathy and encephalopathy characterised by multidomain cognitive and behavioral dysfunction 20 days after the third teprotumumab infusion, with an admission MMSE score of 15. Contrast-enhanced brain MRI, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) examination, and autoimmune encephalitis antibody screening were unremarkable. Prolonged video-electroencephalography(VEEG) demonstrated intermittent diffuse bilateral slow-wave activity, and widespread interictal epileptiform discharges without electrographic or clinical seizures. Systematic workup excluded infectious, autoimmune, metabolic, and psychiatric encephalopathy. Treatment-emergent hyperglycemia was confirmed as a secondary aggravating factor rather than the primary cause. The patient achieved prominent neurological recovery after individualized treapeutic plasma exchange (TPE) and symptomatic management, with a Naranjo score of 6 indicating probable drug-related encephalopathy. NARRATIVE LITERATURE REVIEW: Synthesis of all three previously published teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy cases demonstrates that neurological toxicity can develop as early as the third infusion cycle. These cases present consistent neuroimaging features with variable CSF profiles, and thyroid autoantibody elevation predominantly reflects pre-existing Graves' disease-related autoimmune background instead of directly mediating neurotoxicity. TPE generally facilitates neurological recovery but may be complicated by procedural hypersensitivity and fluctuating therapeutic responses. Notably, interictal epileptiform discharges on VEEG represent a newly identified neurophysiological manifestation of this rare adverse event. A three-hit interacting pathogenic hypothesis is proposed: impaired IGF-1R-dependent neurotrophic signalling serves as the core injury mechanism, while underlying thyroid autoimmunity and treatment-related metabolic disturbance act as susceptibility-modifying factors. DISCUSSION: This case expands the ethnic and clinical spectrum of teprotumumab-related encephalopathy. Diffuse interictal epileptiform discharges without ictal changes reflect reactive cortical hyperexcitability secondary to acute encephalopathy. Metabolic disturbance merely exacerbated neurological impairment. Limitations include single-case constraints, simultaneous multi-intervention therapy, and lack of direct intracranial mechanistic evidence. CONCLUSION: Teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy is a rare but serious adverse event. Close neurological surveillance and are essential during medication. Accumulated cases are needed to establish unified diagnostic and therapeutic criteria.
Phenytoin Toxicity Presenting With Ataxia and Nystagmus: A Case Report.浏览
PMID 42991811 · Cureus · 2026 11 04
作者：Elashmawy S, Kingston K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Phenytoin has a narrow therapeutic window, and elderly patients with multiple comorbidities are more susceptible to developing toxicity. This report details a patient who developed cerebellar symptoms during admission. Elevated phenytoin levels were identified and were higher still when corrected for low serum albumin. Structural, central infective, and epileptic causes were excluded. Despite repeated dose adjustments, therapeutic levels could not be maintained. Establishing therapeutic levels may have been further complicated by declining renal function and concurrent infection, and phenobarbitone levels were also supratherapeutic. He deteriorated and subsequently died. This case highlights the need for early phenytoin level monitoring when toxicity is suspected, particularly in frail patients with hypoalbuminemia, renal impairment, or acute illness, where dose titration is challenging.
The SIK1-NR4A2 axis: a core driver of epilepsy identified by integrated machine learning and experimental validation.推荐
PMID 42991733 · Frontiers in cell and developmental biology · 2026 10 22
作者：Liu X, Zheng S, Yang K, Sun L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy remains a major clinical challenge, suggesting that mechanisms beyond the classical excitation-inhibition imbalance contribute to epilepsy. The Liver kinase B1 (LKB1) signaling pathway coordinates energy homeostasis and cell survival, but its key downstream effectors in epilepsy remain largely unknown. METHODS: We integrated machine learning screening of LKB1 pathway genes using public Gene Expression Omnibus datasets (GSE143272) with in vivo and in vitro experimental validation. A PTZ-induced status epilepticus rat model, AAV-mediated gene knockdown/overexpression, lentiviral transfection of primary hippocampal neurons, Electroencephalography recording, whole-cell patch-clamp recording, and Western blot analysis were employed to investigate the functional role and molecular mechanisms of candidate genes. RESULTS: Machine learning identified SIK1 (salt-inducible kinase 1) as the top-ranked epilepsy risk gene within the LKB1 pathway. SIK1 expression was significantly upregulated in the hippocampus of SE rats. AAV-mediated SIK1 knockdown prolonged seizure latency and reduced seizure duration, while SIK1 overexpression exacerbated seizure severity. Mechanistically, through PPI network analysis and mediation analysis, we identified NR4A2 as a downstream effector of SIK1. SIK1 negatively regulated NR4A2 expression: SIK1 knockdown increased NR4A2, whereas SIK1 overexpression decreased NR4A2. Rescue experiments showed that co-overexpression of SIK1 and NR4A2 reversed the pro-epileptic effect of SIK1 alone. Whole-cell patch-clamp recordings demonstrated that SIK1 knockdown or NR4A2 overexpression restored action potential amplitude to near-normal levels in magnesium-free-treated neurons. CONCLUSION: SIK1 negatively regulates NR4A2, and restoring NR4A2 expression counteracts SIK1-induced neuronal hyperexcitability. These findings suggest that targeting the SIK1-NR4A2 axis may represent a potential therapeutic strategy for epilepsy.
Clinical characteristics, semiology, and comorbidity patterns in non-epileptic seizures (NES): a retrospective comparative study.推荐
PMID 42991691 · Frontiers in psychology · 2026 10 22
作者：Caruso P, Quagliotto M, Radin Y, Pavan S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: Non-epileptic seizures (NES) are paroxysmal events resembling epileptic seizures but not explained by ictal activity. Differential diagnosis may be challenging, particularly because NES and epilepsy can coexist. We aimed to characterize the demographic, clinical, semiological, psychiatric, and instrumental profile of patients with NES, compare patients with NES only with patients with NES epilepsy-only, and assess the robustness of the main associations through sensitivity analyses. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective observational comparative study using a clinical database from our Neurology Unit (2022-2025) including 38 patients with NES and 23 patients with epilepsy without NES. A total of 18 NES patients had comorbid epilepsy; therefore, the primary comparison included mutually exclusive groups: NES-only (n = 20) and epilepsy-only (n = 23). All NES patients were retained for descriptive analyses. Between-group comparisons used exact or non-parametric tests with Benjamini-Hochberg false-discovery-rate (BH-FDR) correction. Sensitivity analyses restricted NES-only patients to those with database-recorded video-EEG confirmation and used Firth penalized logistic regression adjusted for age and sex. RESULTS: Among 38 patients with NES, 94.7% had documented psychological/psychiatric comorbidity, mainly anxiety (50.0%) and depression (28.9%). Positive motor phenomena were documented in 73.7%, neurological examination inconsistency/incongruity in 85.7% of evaluable patients, and video-EEG confirmation in 73.7%. Psychological/psychiatric comorbidity was significantly more frequent in NES-only than epilepsy-only patients (90.0% vs. 13.0%; exact OR 50.40, 95% CI 7.29-670.28; BH-FDR p < 0.001), as was anxiety (45.0% vs. 4.3%; BH-FDR p = 0.014). Broad seizure-semiology domains were not significantly different after multiplicity correction. Among imaged patients, abnormal MRI findings were more frequent in epilepsy-only patients (12/18 vs. 0/10; BH-FDR p = 0.006), although MRI ascertainment differed between groups. The psychiatric-comorbidity association persisted in sensitivity analyses. CONCLUSION: NES showed substantial clinical heterogeneity and frequently coexisted with epilepsy. Psychological/psychiatric comorbidity was markedly more frequent in NES-only than epilepsy-only patients and remained robust in sensitivity analyses, although differential ascertainment limits interpretation. Broad seizure manifestations alone did not distinguish the groups after multiplicity correction. Instrumental findings should be considered descriptive rather than NES biomarkers. These findings support explicit stratification for comorbid epilepsy and standardized prospective assessment of psychiatric and semiological features.
Topiramate-Induced Fanconi Syndrome: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 42991482 · Kidney medicine · 2026 09 09
作者：Berlinkova D, Rohatgi R, Rohatgi R, Jiatu AZ
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Topiramate (TPM), originally approved in 1996 for epilepsy, is now widely administered to prevent migraines, induce weight loss, and suppress alcohol cravings. Common renal adverse side effects include metabolic acidosis and nephrolithiasis; however, acute kidney injury (AKI) is uncommon or underreported. In this report, we present a case of a 41-year-old man with a history of hypertension, obesity, tonic-clonic seizures, and migraines who developed AKI associated with Fanconi syndrome while administered TPM 100 mg daily for migraine prevention. The Naranjo Adverse Drug Reaction Probability Scale, a measure of the likelihood that a drug is causing an adverse reaction, indicated that TPM probably (score of 8) caused AKI with Fanconi syndrome. After supportive therapy and drug cessation the patient's kidney function normalized and the Fanconi syndrome resolved. This case is important for several reasons: (1) this is the third case of AKI and second case associated with Fanconi syndrome, which implies a relationship with TPM, (2) the cases illustrate that early recognition and discontinuation of TPM resolves AKI, and (3) common elements between the cases relay plausible risk factors. Thus, clinicians who prescribe TPM should consider monitoring patients for AKI and electrolyte abnormalities.
Autism Spectrum Disorder in Hypothalamic Hamartoma: Early Developmental Trajectory, Symptom Profile, and Relevance for Autism Research.推荐
PMID 42991107 · Case reports in pediatrics · 2026 11 04
作者：Augé P, Mansat C, Delplancq G, Samara-Boustani D et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Focal brain lesions occurring during critical developmental periods can shed light on the neural substrates underlying autistic symptoms. Hypothalamic hamartomas (HH) are rare congenital malformations that are typically characterized by pharmacoresistant epilepsy, endocrine disturbances, and behavioral or psychiatric disorders. Although autistic symptoms have occasionally been reported in children with HH, they remain poorly described and are rarely documented with standardized diagnostic tools. We present a case report of a 3-year-old boy with HH who was followed from 2 years of age. The report combines detailed early neurodevelopmental history, neuroimaging data, and standardized autism assessments. The child exhibited global developmental delay, early intense sensory features, and marked social communication deficits. The first developmental difficulties preceded the onset of seizures. At 29 months of age, magnetic resonance imaging identified a small intrahypothalamic hamartoma. At 36 months, the Autism Diagnostic Observation Schedule-2 and the Autism Diagnostic Interview-Revised confirmed the diagnosis of autism spectrum disorder (ASD). A comparison with 3 subjects with ASD matched for sex, chronological age, and developmental age showed that the child presented less severe deficits in sharing enjoyment and less intense unusual preoccupations than age-matched autistic peers. This case highlights the importance of systematic ASD screening in children with HH, allowing timely referral to evidence-based early interventions. Moreover, HH may offer a valuable framework for investigating early neurodevelopmental mechanisms associated with ASD, while highlighting the potential contribution of the hypothalamus to early autism mechanisms.
Case Report: Dental management of an 8-year-old child with alternating hemiplegia of childhood under general anesthesia: clinical challenges and multidisciplinary considerations.推荐
PMID 42990973 · Frontiers in dental medicine · 2026 10 14
作者：B Thomas A, Enchackal SM, Shenoy S, Rao MS et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Alternating Hemiplegia of Childhood (AHC) is an ultra-rare neurodevelopmental disorder caused by pathogenic ATP1A3 variants, characterized by recurrent hemiplegic episodes, dystonia, autonomic dysfunction, and intellectual disability. The dental environment represents a recognized trigger for paroxysmal episodes, rendering routine outpatient care unfeasible in affected children. CASE DESCRIPTION: An 8-year-old male with a confirmed de novo ATP1A3 variant presented with extensive dental disease including a grossly decayed tooth 26, multiple carious lesions, retained root stumps, and generalized poor oral hygiene. Comprehensive dental rehabilitation was performed under general anesthesia following multidisciplinary fitness clearance. Extractions, restorations, and scaling were completed without intraoperative or postoperative hemiplegic, seizure, or autonomic events. The patient recovered uneventfully following 24-hour PICU observation. CONCLUSIONS: In this child with genetically confirmed AHC, an individualised, multidisciplinary total-intravenous-anaesthesia protocol allowed comprehensive dental rehabilitation to be completed without hemiplegic, seizure, or autonomic complications. This single case suggests that such a protocol can be safe in AHC, although the disease burden observed underscores the need for earlier preventive dental engagement and caregiver education in this population.

摘要：INTRODUCTION: Overdose of valproic acid, an antiepileptic medication prescribed for seizure disorders, bipolar disorder, and migraine prophylaxis, is common but underrepresented in medical school curricula. Because valproic acid is widely prescribed and toxicity can occur at both therapeutic and supratherapeutic doses, medical students need a foundation in recognizing and managing it. To close this gap, we built a multimodal educational intervention combining didactic instruction with an experiential simulation. METHODS: Fourth-year toxicology elective students completed a knowledge pretest and a confidence-based Likert questionnaire before attending a lecture, then worked in small groups through a 15-minute simulated encounter with a patient with acute-on-chronic valproic acid overdose, followed by a 15-minute debrief. Reactions to the workshop were favorable, and most learners reported intent to change practice. DISCUSSION: This multimodal simulation was an effective way to teach valproic acid overdose management.

发表形式：综述
摘要：Genomic testing is being recognized as an integral part of the management of individuals with epilepsy. However, despite the increasing utility of genomic testing in epilepsy, a substantial proportion of patients who are suspected to have a monogenic cause do not receive a molecular diagnosis. Reanalyzing existing genomic data increases the molecular diagnostic yield over time. As our understanding of the molecular components of epilepsy is changing faster than that of many other conditions, the re-analysis of existing genomic data from patients with epilepsy holds considerable potential for improving diagnostic outcomes.
