Determinants of surgical morbidity following glioblastoma resection: an 839-patient contemporary neurosurgical cohort.浏览
PMID 42975712 · Neurosurgical review · 2026 11 03
作者：Al Helou E, Elia A, Hudelist B, Demasi M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：In glioblastoma surgery, neurosurgeons must balance the oncologic benefit of maximal safe cytoreduction against the risk of postoperative morbidity. We evaluated surgery-related complications, unplanned readmission, and functional outcomes, and assessed whether reoperation, awake mapping, or greater extent of resection increase morbidity. We performed a retrospective single-center study of 839 adults who underwent supratentorial glioblastoma resections (first-line or recurrent) between 2000 and 2024. Primary endpoints were regional or systemic complications within 90 days, unplanned readmission within 30 days, ≥ 30-point KPS score decline, and KPS score < 70 at three postoperative months. Multivariable logistic regression identified independent predictors of postoperative morbidity, with particular focus on surgical strategies (reoperation, awake mapping, extent of resection). Surgery-related morbidity was infrequent. Across all models, increased odds of complications, readmission, or functional decline were independently associated with older age, frailty, baseline KPS score < 70, larger tumor volume, midline mass effect, multilobar involvement, tumor-related epilepsy, and preoperative neurological deficit. In contrast, reoperation, awake craniotomy, and greater extent of resection were not associated with higher complications, readmission, or functional decline rates. In this large contemporary cohort, durable postoperative morbidity was determined primarily by baseline physiological reserve (frailty, low KPS score, preoperative deficits) and disease burden (volume, multilobar spread, midline crossing), whereas reoperation, awake mapping, and maximal resection were not. These findings support frailty-informed selection and function-preserving maximal safe resection to preserve independence, enable timely adjuvant therapy, and ultimately improve survival in glioblastoma patients.
Triphasic waves in leptomeningeal metastases and cycling alternating pattern of encephalopathy during sleep.推荐
PMID 42975624 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 03
作者：Gélisse P, Crespel A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Triphasic waves (TWs), now termed generalized periodic discharges with triphasic morphology, may be mistaken for nonconvulsive status epilepticus (NCSE), particularly in critically ill patients. Changes in vigilance over time can help distinguish encephalopathic patterns from ictal activity. CASE PRESENTATION: A 69-year-old woman with breast cancer and leptomeningeal metastases developed progressive mutism and impaired consciousness. Cerebrospinal fluid analysis showed atypical cells and markedly elevated protein, while brain MRI demonstrated bilateral sulcal hyperintensities, most prominent in the posterior regions. An EEG performed 1 week after bevacizumab and paclitaxel were discontinued showed only mild background slowing without epileptiform discharges. A repeat EEG 1 week later showed nearly continuous TWs. The discharges disappeared during spontaneous sleep and recurred periodically, consistent with a cyclic alternating pattern of encephalopathy during sleep (CAPE). In the absence of significant metabolic and toxic derangements, these features favored an encephalopathic rather than ictal pattern. Given the advanced disease and poor prognosis, care remained supportive and palliative. CONCLUSION: This case highlights the importance of interpreting TWs within their clinical context and in relation to the state of vigilance. Their disappearance at sleep onset, with periodic recurrence during sleep producing a CAPE, supports an encephalopathic rather than ictal interpretation, helping to avoid misdiagnosis of NCSE and unnecessary administration of antiseizure medications.
Do preclinical seizure models predict success or failure in anti-seizure drug development? A retrospective analysis of 89 compounds with divergent clinical outcomes for epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42975562 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 03
作者：Löscher W, Barker-Haliski M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Anti-seizure medication (ASM) discovery remains a major challenge despite over 30 commercially available options. One persistent obstacle for any promising candidate compound to successfully move from preclinical study to clinical approval has been the ambiguity in preclinical model selection. Which models are most predictive of clinical response and failure? For decades, the maximal electroshock seizure (MES), subcutaneous pentylenetetrazole (s.c. PTZ) seizure, 6-Hz psychomotor seizure, and audiogenic seizure tests in rodents have served as foundational ASM screening models. These tests are generally regarded as suitable models of generalized tonic-clonic seizures (MES), non-convulsive (absence) seizures (PTZ), pharmacoresistant focal seizures (6-Hz test), and brain penetrance and generalized convulsive seizures (audiogenic seizures). Together, these assays have supported high-throughput ASM discovery for decades; however, they primarily measure acute anti-seizure response. Furthermore, they do not model the pathophysiology of epilepsy as a chronic human brain disorder. Critically, as shown here, these models do not accurately predict human treatment-resistant focal epilepsy success potential. The amygdala-kindled rat model (AKM) is a relevant human temporal lobe epilepsy model that is well suited to investigational ASM discovery and has been implemented for such use for decades. We hypothesized that AKM would be the most predictive of clinical success and failure among the widely implemented rodent screening models. Our present analysis of 89 ASMs and investigational new drugs (INDs) thus aimed to define which preclinical signals, if any, in the standard rodent seizure models would best predict clinical outcomes. The primary efficacy endpoint in AKM has traditionally been convulsive seizure suppression or electrographic outcomes (afterdischarge duration), with few groups reporting IND effects on focal seizures. In our retrospective analysis of 89 compounds, we find that the AKM focal seizure endpoint was the most predictive of clinical success. This structured retrospective comparative analysis of ASM and IND responses demonstrates the exceptional predictive validity of the AKM for early ASM discovery for treatment-resistant human focal epilepsy.
Pediatric Epilepsy Surgery: Global Survey of Surgical Approaches.推荐
PMID 42975430 · Neurosurgery · 2026 11 03
作者：Ramantani G, Englot D, Cserpan D, Aparicio J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Pediatric epilepsy surgery is an established treatment, but current global data on surgical practices are limited. This study, conducted by the International League Against Epilepsy Pediatric Epilepsy Surgery Taskforce, provides an updated international overview of surgical approaches and their regional variation. METHODS: Group-level data were collected from 61 epilepsy surgery programs (49 pediatric-only) across 29 countries on 6 continents. All children and adolescents who underwent epilepsy surgery in 2023 were included. The study was designed to enable direct comparison with the similar survey conducted by the International League Against Epilepsy in 2004. RESULTS: The cohort comprised 2427 patients. Resections accounted for 50.6% and disconnections for 23.0% of procedures. Resection rates were highest in Oceania (87.3%) and lowest in North America (40.3%). Resections were 43.0% unilobar-most commonly temporal (39.6%) and frontal (32.7%)-and 9.7% multilobar, with 61.9% involving the posterior quadrant. Hemispheric surgeries comprised 12.9% of all cases (highest in South America: 18.1%). Subcortical procedures (eg, for hypothalamic hamartomas or deep-seated lesions) represented 2.7% (highest in South America: 12.1%). Intraoperative electrocorticography was used in 38.3% of cases, most commonly in Oceania (84.1%) and Asia (85.5%). Minimally invasive techniques were used in 7.7% of cases, with the highest rate in North America (16.9%). Laser interstitial thermal therapy was most common (4.9%), followed by radiofrequency ablation (2.1%), endoscopic surgery (0.7%), and radiosurgery (0.1%). Corpus callosotomy was performed in 5.9% of all cases, and no multiple subpial transections were reported. Neurostimulation was used in 14.7% of patients, highest in North America (35.6%), and included vagal nerve stimulation (11.8%), deep brain stimulation (1.5%), and responsive neurostimulation (1.4%). CONCLUSION: Resections and disconnections, including extensive multilobar and hemispheric procedures, remain the core procedures of pediatric epilepsy surgery. Although minimally invasive and neuromodulatory techniques are increasingly used in selected regions-particularly North America-their global adoption remains limited.
Sphenoidal Meningioma with Focal Seizures Masquerading as Immune Reconstitution Inflammatory Syndrome in HIV Patient.推荐
PMID 42975236 · The Nigerian postgraduate medical journal · 2026 11 02
作者：Msigwa SS, Kihaga G
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Seizures in adults with HIV can result from structural lesions, opportunistic infections, immune reconstitution inflammatory syndrome (IRIS) or medication effects, making accurate diagnosis challenging. We report a 47-year-old woman with HIV who developed recurrent focal-onset seizures evolving to bilateral tonic-clonic seizures beginning approximately 4 weeks after initiation of antiretroviral therapy (ART). Neurological examination was unremarkable. Electroencephalography revealed focal slowing over the left anterior temporal region without generalised epileptiform discharges. Targeted infectious testing, including Toxoplasma gondii , Cryptococcus neoformans and Mycobacterium tuberculosis , was negative. Brain magnetic resonance imaging performed at the time of evaluation demonstrated a well-circumscribed, extra-axial mass over the left sphenoid wing measuring 3.0 cm × 4.3 cm, isointense on T1-and T2-weighted sequences, with vasogenic oedema and midline shift, consistent with meningioma. Seizure control was achieved with antiseizure medication; however, definitive treatment required gross total surgical resection. Following surgery, the patient remained seizure-free at 12 months of follow-up. Although ART initiation initially suggested central nervous system IRIS, preserved immune function, negative infectious workup and characteristic imaging confirmed a structural aetiology. This case highlights the importance of a broad differential diagnosis and integrating clinical, electrophysiological, infectious and imaging data to identify treatable structural lesions in adults with HIV.
Neuropsychiatric Features of Individuals With LGI1-Antibody Encephalitis.推荐
PMID 42975090 · The Journal of neuropsychiatry and clinical neurosciences · 2026 11 03
作者：Saini A, Kelleher E, Burchill E, Costello H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Leucine-rich glioma-inactivated 1 antibody (LGI1-Ab) encephalitis is one of the most common forms of autoimmune encephalitis and is associated with prominent psychiatric features. Psychiatric phenotyping of this condition has relied on small studies and has lacked detail. METHODS: In this retrospective study, the authors reviewed the psychiatric symptomatology and clinical course among patients with a confirmed diagnosis of LGI1-Ab encephalitis. RESULTS: The mean±SD age of the study sample (N=31) was 60.7±14.2 years; 71% of patients were men. The time to diagnosis was 4.9±5.8 months, and the follow-up time was 55.8±36.7 months. Eighteen patients had documented hyponatremia, and 22 were treated as inpatients. Psychiatric (71%), cognitive (81%), and neurological (84%) symptoms were present at similar rates, despite 97% of the patients coming from neurology referrals. Patients had a diverse range of seizures, including faciobrachial dystonic seizures. Of 19 patients undergoing detailed psychometric analysis, impairments were noted in memory or orientation (100%), executive function (63%), naming (47%), processing speed (37%), attention (32%), verbal fluency (26%), language (11%), and visuospatial abilities (11%). Patients had psychotic symptoms (42%), agitation or aggression (39%), emotional lability (35%), anxiety (26%), and subjective experiential phenomena (16%). Many patients had psychiatric sequelae that resolved after treatment, but a modest proportion of patients experienced ongoing apathy (6%), behavioral disinhibition (13%), obsessive-compulsive disorder (10%), subjective experiential phenomena (13%), and sleep disorders (29%). CONCLUSIONS: These findings show that cognitive impairment and psychopathology are frequent in LGI1-Ab encephalitis. Future research could examine neuroimaging, psychiatric symptoms, and cognitive deficits in further detail.
The challenge of drug-related seizures: is identifying the cause only part of the battle?推荐

Neural circuits, cognition, and behavior in diffuse glioma.推荐
PMID 42974951 · Neuro-oncology · 2026 11 03
作者：Fageiry S, Hervey-Jumper SL
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Diffuse gliomas arise within and become functionally integrated into neural circuits, establishing bidirectional interactions between malignant cells and the surrounding brain. Neuronal activity promotes glioma growth and progression, while glioma cells remodel the excitability, connectivity, and functional organization of the neural circuits they infiltrate. Pathological excitation-inhibition imbalance can impair cortical information processing, while tumor infiltration simultaneously disrupts, preserves, and induces adaptive reorganization of distributed functional networks. Language processing provides a well defined framework for examining these competing effects because language processing depends on distributed cortical and subcortical networks whose organization can be interrogated using neuroimaging, electrophysiology, and direct cortical stimulation. The pattern and severity of glioma-associated language dysfunction reflect the interaction among tumor location, growth kinetics, molecular and histopathological features, white matter involvement, and the capacity for adaptive redistribution of function within ipsilateral and contralateral networks. Slowly growing gliomas can permit substantial functional reorganization, whereas rapidly infiltrating tumors may constrain the time and biological substrate available for effective network adaptation. In this review, we examine reciprocal glioma-neural circuit interactions, with particular emphasis on the mechanisms of glioma-associated neuronal hyperexcitability and the disruption, preservation, and adaptive remodeling of language networks.
Cerebrospinal Fluid GAD65-Antibody Levels Accurately Identify Relevant Neurological Diseases.浏览
PMID 42974934 · Annals of clinical and translational neurology · 2026 11 03
作者：Herman MC, Marzello M, Tharwani ZH, Kotaru S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Retrospective cohort study to investigate which factors most reliably distinguish incidental GAD65-antibodies (iGAD65) from true GAD65-antibody neurological disease (tGAD65). 167 adults with positive serum and CSF GAD65-antibodies (> 0.02 nmol/L by radioimmunoassay) were classified as tGAD65 or iGAD65, with diagnostic performance of paraclinical parameters compared in discovery and validation cohorts. CSF GAD65-antibody titers ≥ 1 nmol/L performed best in discovery (AUC 0.956) and validation (AUC 0.949; p = 0.814), with 89.7% sensitivity and 94% specificity for identifying tGAD65. Serum titers > 20 nmol/L and indices of intrathecal synthesis showed lower discriminative ability. Our finding aims to reduce misdiagnosis and identify potential immunotherapy-responsive diseases.
Not "just in the head": psychosis triggered by chronic subdural hematoma.推荐
PMID 42974826 · Journal of neurosurgical sciences · 2026 Oct
作者：Sollenberger CH, Bediako I, Hoglund Z, Kathawate VG et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Chronic subdural hematoma (cSDH) is a common and increasingly prevalent neurosurgical condition in older adults, typically presenting with headaches, cognitive decline, or focal neurological deficits. Neuropsychiatric manifestations such as confusion, memory impairment, and personality change are well recognized. However, frank psychosis - characterized by hallucinations, delusions, or grossly disorganized behavior - is a rare and underappreciated presentation. When psychosis occurs in older patients, symptoms are frequently misattributed to primary psychiatric or neurodegenerative disorders, leading to delayed diagnosis and treatment. This narrative review examines the relationship between cSDH and psychosis, synthesizing historical and contemporary literature to highlight clinical patterns, mechanisms, and management considerations. Available evidence, largely from case reports and small series, suggests that psychosis due to cSDH most often occurs in older individuals without prior psychiatric history and may present without focal neurological deficits. Symptom resolution following hematoma evacuation supports a causal relationship. Proposed mechanisms include mass effect, intracranial hypertension, cerebral hypoperfusion inflammatory cytokine-mediated cortical dysfunction, and seizure-related phenomena. Management centers on prompt neurosurgical intervention, most commonly burr-hole evacuation, which frequently leads to rapid improvement of psychiatric symptoms. Supportive psychopharmacologic measures may be used transiently but should not obscure neurological assessment. Emerging nonsurgical therapies, including statins and middle meningeal artery embolization, may play adjunctive roles but are not substitutes for urgent decompression. This review emphasizes maintaining a high index of suspicion for cSDH in older patients with new-onset psychosis and the need for timely neuroimaging, as cSDH is a treatable and potentially reversible cause of severe psychiatric symptoms.
An Evolving Neuropsychiatric Syndrome Following Liver Transplant: Trauma-Related Symptoms, Functional Neurologic Disorder, and Delirium.浏览
PMID 42974670 · Harvard review of psychiatry · 2026 11 03
作者：Rivera Criado MJ, Vivoni Corominas S, Rivas-Grajales AM, Rojas-Velasquez D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：This case describes an adult liver transplant recipient who developed a diagnostically challenging neuropsychiatric syndrome in the early postoperative period. His presentation included new-onset tremors, visual and auditory hallucinations, seizure-like episodes with variable semiology, and a fluctuating cognitive exam in the context of immunosuppressant exposure, medical complications, polypharmacy, and preexisting psychiatric vulnerability. Early concerns included medication-induced neurotoxicity, steroid-related mood symptoms, and functional neurologic symptoms. Over time, a more integrated formulation emerged, encompassing post-transplant delirium, functional neurologic disorder (FND), and trauma-related distress. This case illustrates the difficulty of distinguishing toxic-metabolic, structural, and functional etiologies in complex medical settings. In particular, we explore managing new onset FND in the hospital setting. Discussants explore the neurobiology and management of post-transplant delirium, the role of medical trauma in functional symptom emergence, and the neuropsychiatric interface between FND and PNES, highlighting key considerations for psychiatrists navigating interdisciplinary care.
Get In and Go: Why Health Care Needs the Patient at the Wheel.浏览
PMID 42974636 · Digital biomarkers · 2026 10 10
作者：Cohen AB
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: US health care cannot meet demand. More than 100 million Americans lack basic primary care; shortages of over 80,000 physicians and 100,000 nurses are projected by the late 2030s; wait times keep rising; and roughly a quarter of national health spending is considered waste. Most digital health and artificial intelligence (AI) investment aims to make clinicians and health systems marginally more efficient, which cannot close a gap this large. Patients themselves can still manage only low-risk conditions, using over-the-counter products and consumer devices that gather data but rarely turn it into action. SUMMARY: This article introduces Personal Health Care, a condition-agnostic and AI-enabled system architecture for what patients can do for themselves between clinical encounters, or instead of them, from prevention to treatment, and across the risk spectrum. Four conditions now make it feasible: system strain, generative AI that has reached clinical utility, patient-generated health data, and available capital. The architecture rests on five capabilities: sensing; deciding and acting; monitoring, governance, and safety; escalation; and external integration. A case of a poststroke patient with a possible breakthrough seizure shows the difference between silence between visits and continuous presence. The article also asks whether such systems should be measured against expert gold standards or against usual care, and introduces the series of articles, each taking up one capability. KEY MESSAGES: Provider-facing technology alone will not close the access and cost gap, so patients need capabilities they control. Personal Health Care is a system architecture rather than a single product, defined by five required capabilities. The limiting factors are validation, governance, liability, and reimbursement, not model capability.
Exploring ifosfamide-related central nervous system adverse events based on the FAERS database and clinical feature analysis of cases.浏览
PMID 42974612 · Frontiers in oncology · 2026 10 20
作者：Shi P, Mei Q, Zheng R, Wang S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Ifosfamide, an alkylating agent used to treat various malignancies, has limited real-world safety data on neurotoxicity. Therefore, this study analyzed ifosfamide-related Central Nervous System Adverse Events (CNS AEs) to provide a reference for drug risk management. METHODS: The disproportionality study was performed leveraging the FDA Adverse Event Reporting System (FAERS) database from 2004 to 2024. Reporting Odds Ratio (ROR), Proportional Reporting Ratio (PRR), Multi-item Gamma Poisson Shrinker (MGPS), and Bayesian Information Component (IC) were applied to identify and evaluate potential CNS AEs associated with ifosfamide. The Weibull distribution was applied to characterize the risk of AEs over time. Case reports from PubMed and SpringerLink were reviewed to summarize the clinical characteristics of these AEs. RESULTS: This study identified 786 reports of ifosfamide-related CNS AEs. Disproportionality analysis revealed encephalopathy as the strongest signal (n=252, ROR = 101.19). Six label-unlisted adverse events were also identified, including brain oedema, hydrocephalus, spinal cord compression, cerebral ischemia, gaze palsy, and Guillain-Barré syndrome. The time-to-onset analysis revealed an early failure pattern with a median onset of 2 days after drug exposure. Brain oedema exhibited the highest mortality rate (83.33%). Subgroup analysis suggested greater susceptibility to encephalopathy in female patients and to confusion and coma in elderly patients. The systematic case review included 61 cases from 42 articles, involving patients aged 3-77 years. Therein, 46 (75.41%) cases experienced central neurotoxic reactions after receiving one to three cycles of ifosfamide-containing chemotherapy and the common clinical symptoms included impaired consciousness, hallucinations, seizures, delirium, disorientation, and various psychiatric or behavioral abnormalities. CONCLUSIONS: This study corroborates clinical observations by systematically characterizing the features of ifosfamide-related CNS toxicity and identifying potentially label-unlisted AEs, supporting further validation in prospective epidemiological studies.
Unexplained neurological deterioration after acute brain injury: A structured intensive care framework for targeted continuous electroencephalogram.推荐
PMID 42974604 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 23
作者：Oda K, Nakatani N, Kinoshita T, Fukuda A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurological deterioration after acute brain injury is common in the intensive care unit, yet its cause may remain uncertain when repeat imaging is stable or does not fully explain the examination. Delayed recognition of occult structural, vascular, systemic, metabolic, medication-related, or electrographic mechanisms may postpone time-sensitive treatment. We aimed to provide a structured bedside framework and define the role of targeted continuous electroencephalography (cEEG). METHODS: We performed a narrative synthesis of evidence and consensus recommendations across aneurysmal subarachnoid hemorrhage, intracerebral hemorrhage, ischemic stroke, traumatic brain injury, and acute subdural hematoma, emphasizing bedside examination, repeat neuroimaging, vascular reassessment, systemic physiology, medication effects, and critical care electroencephalogram (EEG). RESULTS: Deterioration should first be confirmed as a true change and classified as explained, unexplained, disproportionate, or fluctuating/episodic. Evaluation should proceed in parallel: Correct reversible systemic and metabolic derangements, review sedative and analgesic exposure, and reassess structural or vascular complications. cEEG is most informative when deterioration remains unexplained after stabilization, exceeds the apparent imaging burden, fluctuates, follows a clinical seizure, or presents as delayed awakening. It can identify electrographic seizures, status epilepticus, and rhythmic or periodic patterns; however, a nonexplanatory recording neither establishes neurological stability nor excludes nonelectrographic secondary injury. CONCLUSION: A structured intensive care framework may reduce premature diagnostic closure and support timely, selective cEEG. Continuous EEG should complement, not replace, repeated clinical examination, neuroimaging, vascular evaluation, and correction of systemic physiology.
Comparing the safety and efficacy of low-dose versus high-dose tranexamic acid in reducing blood loss in neurosurgical procedures: A systematic review and meta-analysis.浏览
PMID 42974572 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 23
作者：Alqarni GS, Alamer M, Ywsef KA, Faleh MN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Perioperative bleeding is a major concern in neurosurgery, including spine fusions and cranial tumor resections. Tranexamic acid (TXA) is commonly used to reduce blood loss, but the ideal dosing strategy remains controversial. High-dose TXA regimens may enhance hemostasis but raise safety concerns, whereas low-dose regimens are commonly used with unclear comparative efficacy. This review aimed to compare the safety and efficacy of low-dose versus high-dose TXA in neurosurgical procedures. METHODS: A comprehensive search of PubMed, Web of Science, and Google Scholar through January 2026 identified studies comparing TXA dosing in neurosurgery. Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and observational studies reporting at least one relevant outcome were included. RESULTS: Intraoperative blood loss is the primary outcome. Moreover, total blood loss, length of hospital stay, and duration of the surgery were secondary outcomes. The quality of the included studies was assessed using risk of bias 2 for RCTs and Methodological Index for Non-Randomized Studies for non-randomized studies. Meta-analysis was performed where appropriate. Eleven studies met inclusion criteria, with seven included in quantitative synthesis. Both low- and high-dose TXA significantly reduced perioperative blood loss compared with no TXA. However, no statistically significant differences were observed across the outcomes. No increase in thromboembolic events, seizures, or other major complications was observed with high-dose TXA. CONCLUSION: Both dosing strategies of TXA reduced blood loss effectively. Current evidence does not demonstrate a clear advantage of high-dose over low-dose TXA. However, given the limited direct comparative evidence, further high-quality randomized trials are needed to define the optimal dosing strategy.
Atypical symptoms from a small occipital meningioma: A case report.推荐
PMID 42974549 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 30
作者：Haddad R, Chahine S, Saade M, Sett AE et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Meningiomas are typically slow-growing benign intracranial tumors, and symptomatic lesions smaller than 2 cm are uncommon, particularly in immunosuppressed patients, in whom primary central nervous system lymphoma may pose a diagnostic challenge. CASE DESCRIPTION: We report the case of a 39-year-old woman with a history of renal transplantation on immunosuppressive therapy who presented with recurrent headaches, bilateral visual blurring, visual illusions, and later generalized seizures. Ophthalmological assessment revealed a left homonymous hemianopsia. Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) demonstrated a small (1 cm) right occipital extra-axial lesion suggestive of meningioma. Because of her immunosuppressed status, cerebral lymphoma was also considered, since it has a similar morphology on MRI. A 5-day high-dose corticosteroid trial did not modify the lesion, supporting the diagnosis of meningioma. CONCLUSION: This case highlights the diagnostic challenges posed by atypical occipital meningiomas and emphasizes that even very small lesions may produce substantial neurological and neuropsychiatric manifestations.
Calvarial erosion secondary to epithelioid glioblastoma: A molecularly characterized case report.推荐
PMID 42974534 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 16
作者：Khojah AM, Alkhaibary A, AlSufiani F, Alassiri A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epithelioid glioblastoma (eGBM) is a rare, aggressive IDH-wildtype glioblastoma of children and young adults. It is typically B-Raf proto-oncogene, serine/threonine kinase (BRAF) valine-to-glutamic acid substitution at amino acid position 600 (V600E)/ cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitor 2A (CDKN2A)/ cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitor 2B (CDKN2B) -altered. Calvarial erosion with extracranial extension is exceptionally rare and may mimic primary calvarial or extra-axial tumors. CASE DESCRIPTION: A 16-year-old girl presented with a 2-week history of headache and a new-onset generalized tonic-clonic seizure. Neuroimaging revealed a superficially located left temporal intra-axial lesion with heterogeneous enhancement, extensive vasogenic edema, focal dural enhancement, and a through-and-through temporal squamous bone defect suggesting transdural extension. The patient underwent a left temporal craniotomy and tumor resection. The temporal squama was completely eroded, and the tumor breached the dura and extended beyond the inner table, confirming extracranial spread. Gross-total resection, including involved bone and dura with reconstruction, was achieved without postoperative complications or neurological deficit. Histopathology showed eGBM, central nervous system World Health Organization grade 4, with rhabdoid features, necrosis, subpial condensation, and meningeal involvement. Molecular profiling demonstrated BRAF V600E, telomerase reverse transcriptase (TERT) promoter -146C>T, CDKN2A/2B homozygous deletion, and methylthioadenosine phosphorylase (MTAP) loss, with isocitrate dehydrogenase 1 (NADP+) (IDH1)/ isocitrate dehydrogenase 2 (NADP+) (IDH2) and H3F3A wildtype, low tumor mutational burden, and microsatellite stability. Despite standard chemoradiotherapy, the patient passed away 6 months after diagnosis. CONCLUSION: eGBM may present with calvarial erosion and surgically confirmed extracranial extension in pediatric patients. Recognition of skull defects and dural enhancement should prompt planning for en bloc resection of invaded bone and dura with reconstruction and early comprehensive molecular testing to identify actionable targets such as BRAF V600E.
Rationale for giving antiepileptics intraoperatively in patients undergoing surgery for supratentorial brain tumors.推荐
PMID 42974533 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 23
作者：Singh A, Banavath HN, Mohindra S, Javia BV et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Early postoperative seizures remain a frequent complication in supratentorial tumor surgery. There is no standard recommendation for additional intraoperative phenytoin administration. METHODS: One hundred patients with supratentorial brain tumors undergoing surgery were included. All patients received regular phenytoin preoperatively and received an additional dose of phenytoin intraoperatively. Phenytoin levels were measured in preoperative, intraoperative, and postoperative periods. Presence or absence of seizures in the postoperative period was recorded. RESULTS: The mean preoperative phenytoin values were 15.9 ± 2.623, intraoperative phenytoin levels were 11.38 ± 3.667, and the mean postoperative phenytoin levels were 14.03 ± 2.607. Eight patients developed seizures in the postoperative period. The mean difference between intraoperative and postoperative drug levels was significant in the seizure group as compared to the other group. CONCLUSION: Intraoperative phenytoin levels dropped in all patients during surgery, but the drop was significantly higher in patients who developed seizures in the postoperative period. The routine measurement of perioperative plasma phenytoin levels may be associated with the use of adding phenytoin intraoperatively to reduce the incidence of postoperative seizures. The aim of our study was to determine the plasma phenytoin levels in intraoperative period which can determine early postoperative seizure risk.
Awake craniotomy as a routine pathway for supratentorial tumor surgery: A 500-case awake-craniotomy experience with institutional resource and charge benchmarking.浏览
PMID 42974532 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 16
作者：Lovo EE, Rodriguez Santos JA, Cruz C, Chang JE et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Integrated safety, tolerability, and resource data on awake craniotomy in resource-constrained settings are lacking. Improving accessibility has remained central to our program. We report an experience exceeding 500 awake craniotomies with structured comparator synthesis. METHODS: Three nested cohorts from one Central American institution were analyzed: 218 awake craniotomies (2007-2018, audited safety), 500 procedures spanning neoplastic and non-neoplastic lesions (2007-2026, program tolerability), and 30 awake versus 30 contemporaneous general-anesthesia craniotomies (2024-2026, descriptive benchmarking). The two contemporary groups were restricted to elective supratentorial intraparenchymal tumor resections. The contemporary cohort was derived from billing and administrative records; baseline clinical, radiological, and histopathological data were unavailable; groups were managed by different teams. RESULTS: Across the 500-case program, completion without conversion was 99.0%; 30-day mortality, ascertainable only for the 282 registry-era procedures, was 3.2%. In the 218-case cohort, seizures occurred in 5.1%, perioperative mortality was 0.5%, and none required ventilation; extent of resection was 73%, 94%, and 100% in eloquent, adjacent to eloquence, and non-eloquent cortex. In the contemporaneous institutional benchmark, the awake pathway showed shorter stay (1.0 vs. 2.0 days), no intensive-care use (0/30 vs. 9/30), shorter incision-to-closure time, and $5,205 lower median billed charges than the general-anesthesia pathway. Outcomes were consistent with 14 comparator studies. CONCLUSION: In this high-volume, resource-constrained program, to the best of our knowledge, the only protocolized awake-craniotomy program in the region, awake craniotomy served as a routine pathway for eligible supratentorial intraparenchymal lesions, with high completion, favorable safety, short hospitalization, no intensive-care admission, and lower observed billed charges than conventional practice.
Dual parasagittal craniotomy for single-stage resection of non-contiguous falcine meningiomas: A case report.浏览
PMID 42974521 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 09
作者：Mdarhri M, El Ouahabi M, Hmamouche OM, Hammoud M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: The management of multiple falcine meningiomas located in non-contiguous regions remains challenging, with no established consensus regarding the optimal surgical strategy. Single-stage resection may be limited by surgical access, whereas staged procedures increase overall treatment burden. CASE DESCRIPTION: We report the case of a 44-year-old man with no significant medical history presenting with progressive right-sided weakness and a generalized seizure. Imaging revealed two spatially distinct falcine lesions located at the anterior and posterior aspects of the middle third of the falx. A single-stage resection was performed using a dual parasagittal craniotomy ("dual window approach"), allowing direct and independent access to each lesion while preserving the superior sagittal sinus. Gross total resection was achieved, corresponding to Simpson grade II for the anterior lesion due to sinus involvement and Simpson grade I for the posterior lesion. The postoperative course was uneventful, with improvement of the neurological deficit. CONCLUSION: The dual-window parasagittal approach represents a practical option for selected cases of non-contiguous falcine meningiomas, enabling safe single-stage resection while minimizing venous risk and avoiding extensive exposure. Further studies are needed to define its role in comparison with conventional strategies.
Psychedelics in neurosurgery: Potential utility in prevention, perioperative care, pain management, and neurorehabilitation.浏览
PMID 42974518 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 09
作者：Garcia-Bitar A, Castañeda EJ, Buenrostro-Rangel JR, Vázquez-Flores AK et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurosurgical patients carry a high and frequently undertreated burden of psychological suffering, acute and chronic pain, and neurological deficits amenable to recovery. The resurgence of psychedelic research has produced relevant evidence in adjacent fields, but its applicability to neurosurgery has not been systematized. This review synthesizes that evidence across prevention, perioperative care, pain management, and neurorehabilitation, and delineates the safety considerations specific to neurosurgery. METHODS: Narrative review structured around a population, intervention, comparator, outcome, and time question, covering 2020-2026. The classic serotonergic psychedelics (psilocybin, lysergic acid diethylamide [LSD], N,N-dimethyltryptamine, ayahuasca, 5-methoxy-N,N-dimethyltryptamine, and mescaline), the iboga alkaloid ibogaine, the entactogen 3,4-methylenedioxymethamphetamine, and the dissociatives ketamine and esketamine were examined. Randomized controlled trials, systematic reviews, meta-analyses, and high-quality mechanistic studies were prioritized. RESULTS: No trial has administered these agents in neurosurgical cohorts, so all evidence is indirect. Psilocybin- assisted therapy produces substantial, durable reductions in cancer-related depression, anxiety, and existential distress. LSD is anxiolytic; parenteral tryptamines act rapidly in depression; ibogaine improved disability, post- traumatic stress, and cognition after traumatic brain injury but carries arrhythmia risk. Perioperative ketamine and esketamine show antidepressant and opioid-sparing effects. Classic psychedelics promote neuroplasticity and are neuroprotective in preclinical models. CONCLUSION: A biologically plausible basis exists for exploring these agents in neurosurgery, but direct evidence is absent and translation is constrained by the seizure threshold, hemodynamic and cerebrovascular effects, perioperative serotonergic interactions, and the need for structured psychological support. Perioperative esketamine and psilocybin for neuro-oncological distress are the most feasible starting points. Purpose-designed neurosurgical trials are required.
An age-related decline in human fluid intelligence corresponds with immature astroglia prevalence.推荐
PMID 42974419 · Frontiers in cellular neuroscience · 2026 10 20
作者：Ammothumkandy A, Corona L, Atai NA, Abedi A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Age-related cognitive decline remains a daunting health challenge, and targeting neuronal cells alone has been insufficient to halt its progression. Astroglia are emerging as key players in cognitive function and dysfunction, yet their relevance to age-associated cognitive decline in humans remains unclear. Epilepsy patients undergo age-amplified progressive cognitive decline. Our prior research in Mesial Temporal Lobe Epilepsy (MTLE) patients identified pathological stellar Dcx + immature astroglia in epileptic, but not healthy hippocampus. In this study, we combined histology data with pre-surgical neuropsychology tests to assess (1) age-associated cognitive decline in MTLE patients (N = 40), (2) age-related changes in Dcx + immature astroglia (N = 29), and (3) the association between Dcx + immature astroglia and cognitive decline (N = 19). We observed a strong decline in fluid intelligence, visuospatial reconstruction, and memory with age. An increase in Dcx + immature astroglia positively correlated with age, whereas negatively associated with nonverbal abstract reasoning and fluid intelligence as measured by the Raven's Progressive Matrices test. These findings reveal an interplay among aging, fluid intelligence decline, and immature astroglia prevalence in epilepsy patients. Our study suggests immature astroglia represent a new cellular target to consider for altering age-associated cognitive decline.
Calcified choroid plexus papilloma of the temporal horn presenting with seizures in an adult without hydrocephalus: An illustrative case.推荐
PMID 42974319 · Surgical neurology international · 2026 10 23
作者：Dover N, McManus R, Britz G, Powell S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Choroid plexus papillomas (CPPs) are rare, benign tumors that typically occur in pediatric patients and result in hydrocephalus. Presentations in an adult featuring seizures, particularly focal impaired awareness seizures, occur infrequently, especially in the absence of hydrocephalus. CASE DESCRIPTION: A 50-year-old female exhibited episodes of temporary unresponsiveness characteristic of a focal impaired awareness seizure. Imaging identified a calcified mesial temporal lesion; electroencephalogram revealed epileptiform discharges. Surgical pathology confirmed the presence of CPP. The patient began treatment with levetiracetam and later underwent surgical removal of the tumor. Postoperatively, the patient experienced no seizures and exhibited no neurological deficits. CONCLUSION: While CPPs usually cause hydrocephalus, this case had no ventricular enlargement on imaging. Instead, a calcified CPP diagnostically mimicked a cavernoma by presenting solely with seizures and a calcified intraventricular temporal-horn mass in a 50-year-old adult; typically, CPPs present in the fourth ventricle rather than the temporal horn, whereas cavernomas regularly present in the temporal lobe with a classically "popcorn-like" appearance. Thus, CPPs can cause focal seizures via local cortical irritation that is calcified, similarly to a cavernoma; CPPs can also present in a middle-aged adult rather than a pediatric patient and lack the most common diagnostic sign of hydrocephalus. The cumulative rarity of a CPP that lacks hydrocephalus, causes seizures, contains calcifications, and manifests later in life makes for an incredibly atypical and unique case of CPP. Without pathology, the given symptoms and imaging were more suggestive of a cavernous malformation rather than a CPP, highlighting the importance of biopsy for an accurate diagnosis; early surgical resection is crucial and may be curative.
A Case of SLE Flare With Class IV Lupus Nephritis and Tuberculous Meningitis Presenting as Neuropsychiatric Symptoms: A Diagnostic Dilemma.推荐
PMID 42974234 · Case reports in rheumatology · 2026 11 02
作者：Bhandari G, Chhetri AK, Gyawali R, Chapagain B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) is a multisystem autoimmune disease with potential neuropsychiatric involvement (NPSLE) and increased susceptibility to opportunistic infections like tuberculosis (TB), particularly in endemic regions such as Nepal where the estimated incidence is 229 per 100,000 population. Differentiating NPSLE from tuberculous meningitis (TBM) is diagnostically challenging due to overlapping clinical and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) findings. CASE PRESENTATION: A 25-year-old male with a 1-year history of SLE presented with facial puffiness, oral ulcers, polyarthritis, lower limb edema, and a 6-week history of renal symptoms. Laboratory investigations revealed microcytic anemia (Hb 7.4 g/dL), nephrotic-range proteinuria (urine protein-to-creatinine ratio 4.8 g/g), strongly positive anti-dsDNA, and low complement levels. ENA panel was positive for anti-Ro/SSA but negative for antiribosomal P and antiphospholipid antibodies. Renal biopsy confirmed class IV lupus nephritis (Activity Index 11/24 and Chronicity Index 3/12). Following intravenous methylprednisolone pulse therapy (500 mg/day for 3 days), he developed generalized tonic-clonic seizures and altered sensorium. EEG revealed epileptiform discharges. CSF analysis showed lymphocytic pleocytosis (500 cells/μL and 80% lymphocytes), elevated protein (578 mg/dL), low glucose (1.4 mmol/L), and elevated ADA (10.9 U/L), suggestive of TBM despite negative GeneXpert MTB/RIF, viral PCR, and cryptococcal antigen assays. MRI brain showed cerebral atrophy, calcified granulomas in the right temporal lobe, and a nonspecific demyelinating white matter lesion. Based on clinical, CSF, and imaging findings, empirical antitubercular therapy (ATT) was initiated alongside tailored immunosuppression. The patient's neurological and renal status markedly improved with complete resolution of seizures. DISCUSSION: Distinguishing NPSLE from CNS infections requires careful evaluation of serial CSF parameters, biomarkers, and MRI findings. In endemic regions, empirical ATT should be initiated early when clinical and laboratory features suggest TBM, even when molecular or culture tests are negative. CONCLUSION: In SLE patients with neuropsychiatric symptoms in TB-endemic areas, clinicians must consider TBM even when definitive microbiological tests are negative. Early empirical treatment can significantly improve patient survival and long-term outcomes.

发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Cerebral phaeohyphomycosis (CPHM) is an uncommon and life-threatening infection caused by a variety of dematiaceous fungi, especially rare in immunocompetent individuals. Due to its nonspecific clinical and radiological presentation, it is frequently misdiagnosed, delaying appropriate treatment and leading to poor outcomes. We aim to describe radiological and histopathological findings of patients with CPHM treated at our institution, highlighting the role of combined aggressive treatment on clinical outcomes. CASE DESCRIPTION: We present two cases of brain phaeohyphomycosis in healthy women, aged 58 and 73 years. The first presented with hemiparesis, headache, and seizure. Case Report: Longitudinal evaluation of cerebrospinal fluid Alzheimer's disease core biomarkers in a case of refractory late-onset epilepsy of unknown etiology.推荐
PMID 42973984 · Frontiers in aging neuroscience · 2026 10 12
作者：Wada T, Kuzuya A, Koda K, Ueda S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：In recent years, late-onset epilepsy of unknown etiology (LOEU) has increasingly been recognized as a risk factor for Alzheimer's disease (AD) and other forms of dementia. We present a case of refractory LOEU with progressive cognitive decline, longitudinally evaluated over more than 20 years using neuropsychological tests, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) AD core biomarkers (Aβ42/40 ratio, p-tau), electroencephalography, and neuroimaging. The CSF AD core biomarkers were consistently positive, indicating the presence of both amyloid and tau pathology; however, CSF Aβ levels markedly decreased in parallel with improved seizure control. Notably, the addition of low-dose perampanel (1 mg/day) led to improvement in both cognitive function and seizure control, while 18F-flutemetamol positron emission tomography remained visually negative despite exceeding the predefined quantitative Centiloid threshold. Two years later, seizure frequency increased again, cognitive function deteriorated, and amyloid PET became visually positive, accompanied by an increase in the quantitative Centiloid value. This longitudinal observation suggests that chronic epileptic activity may modify or confound the interpretation of CSF AD biomarker profiles and contribute to cognitive decline, while concomitant early or slowly progressive AD pathology cannot be excluded. The overall biomarker trajectory, including persistently positive CSF AD biomarkers and progressive amyloid PET findings, is compatible with evolving AD pathology, although the marked decline in absolute CSF Aβ concentrations during improved seizure control suggests that chronic epileptic activity may also influence biomarker dynamics. Careful interpretation of AD biomarkers is therefore essential in patients with LOEU, particularly in the era of anti-amyloid therapies.

摘要：Epilepsy is a neurological disease characterized by an enduring predisposition to recurrent seizures. Approximately 1% of the global population is affected by epilepsy, making it the second most common neurological disease after stroke. Individuals with epilepsy are at heightened risk for both seizure-related and unrelated injuries, including soft tissue injuries, head injury, dental and tongue injury, burns and orthopaedic injuries. Several individual studies on epilepsy and burn injuries have been published from various countries. 发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Sleep-related hypermotor epilepsy (SHE) is a form of focal epilepsy that can be caused by a pathogenic autosomal-dominant DEP domain containing Protein 5 (DEPDC5) variant. Given its variable clinical presentation and etiology, SHE can be challenging to diagnose and treat effectively. This case describes a father-daughter pair who experienced sleep-related seizures with similar semiologies and were later diagnosed with autosomal dominant sleep-related hypermotor epilepsy (ADSHE) after genetic test results revealed that they each had a DEPDC5 deletion in Exon 28. Clinical characteristics associated with GAD65 antibodies: a comparative study between high and low GAD65-ab cohorts.浏览
PMID 42973644 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 19
作者：Lombardo-Del Toro P, Jaraba S, Hernández-Pérez G, Nedkova V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: The frequency of neurological manifestations associated with serum GAD65 antibodies (NeuGAD) varies across studies, and the risk of neurological involvement in patients with high GAD65-ab titers remains unclear. This study aimed to determine the prevalence and clinical characteristics of neurological manifestations in patients with high (≥2,000 IU/mL) versus low (<2,000 IU/mL) serum GAD65-ab titers. METHODS: We compared two cohorts: one cohort of 194 patients with diabetes mellitus (DM) diagnosed between 2000 and 2005 with positive low GAD65-ab titers, and a cohort of 150 patients with high GAD65-ab titers collected between 2013 and 2024. Groups were compared using the Mann-Whitney U test, chi-square/Fisher's exact test, and multivariable logistic regression. RESULTS: Among patients with high GAD65-ab titers, 32/150 (21.3%) had NeuGAD, most frequently presenting as epilepsy (13/32, 40.6%). Of the 128 patients with DM, 17 (13.3%) had NeuGAD. DM was present in 112/118 (94.1%) patients without NeuGAD, with frequencies varying by neurological syndrome without reaching significance. Age at NeuGAD onset was significantly lower in patients with DM than without DM (43.8 ± 13.5 vs. 55.4 ± 14.1 years, p = 0.03). None of the 194 low-titer patients developed NeuGAD (0%; 95% CI 0.0-1.9%). CONCLUSION: NeuGAD developed in 13.3% of DM patients with high GAD65-ab titers, while none of the low-titer patients developed NeuGAD, and it appeared at a younger age in patients with DM. We recommend serial GAD65-ab testing in patients with DM and high GAD65-ab titers, and prompt neurology referral when neurological symptoms arise, to allow earlier NeuGAD diagnosis.
Case Report: Successful treatment of drug-resistant epilepsy with unilateral deep brain stimulation of the anterior nucleus of the thalamus.推荐
PMID 42973508 · Frontiers in neuroscience · 2026 10 19
作者：Sasaki R, Yamada K, Tamura K, Kinoshita M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Deep brain stimulation of the anterior nucleus of the thalamus (ANT-DBS) is an established palliative treatment for drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE) and is conventionally performed bilaterally. However, the efficacy of unilateral ANT-DBS remains unclear. We report a patient with DRE who underwent unilateral ANT-DBS because of an epileptogenic network predominantly involving one side and anatomical constraints precluding electrode implantation on the contralateral side. METHODS: A 29-year-old female underwent comprehensive presurgical evaluation, including seizure semiology assessment, video-electroencephalography (EEG), computed tomography, and magnetic resonance imaging. She developed DRE following multimodal treatment for a suprasellar germ cell tumor during childhood. Despite multiple antiseizure medications and vagus nerve stimulation, she continued to experience approximately 10 habitual seizures per day. RESULTS: Seizure semiology was characterized by asymmetric tonic posturing of the limbs lasting 10-30 s, followed by an atonic phase. EEG findings indicated a widespread epileptogenic network predominantly involving the right frontal and temporal areas. Brain imaging showed severe left hemispheric atrophy, marked widening of the left subdural space, and a pre-existing shunt system, which prevented identification of the left ANT. Therefore, DBS implantation was performed in the right ANT alone. Stimulation was initiated 7 days after surgery at an intensity of 2 mA. Following initiation of right ANT stimulation, seizure frequency decreased from approximately 10 to one per day, likely due to a microlesioning effect. Thereafter, right ANT stimulation was associated with a sustained seizure reduction of approximately 60%. Postoperative EEG demonstrated a reduction in interictal epileptiform discharges. The clinical benefit was maintained at the 9-month follow-up, with no surgery- or stimulation-related adverse events were observed. CONCLUSION: Unilateral ANT-DBS can provide clinically meaningful seizure control in carefully selected patients whose predominant epileptogenic network is localized to one cerebral hemisphere. This approach may represent a feasible therapeutic option when anatomical or surgical constraints prevent conventional bilateral ANT electrode implantation. Further studies with larger patient cohorts and longer follow-up periods are required to clarify the indications, durability, and efficacy of unilateral ANT-DBS.
Pediatric Leptomeningeal Glial Tumors: A Retrospective Case Series and Review of the Literature.浏览
PMID 42973419 · Turkish archives of pediatrics · 2026 10 28
作者：Şahin S, Alçi M, Kaygusuz Aydemir B, Sargin F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Diffuse leptomeningeal glioneuronal tumor (DLGNT), including primary diffuse leptomeningeal oligodendroglial tumors (PDLOs) and leptomeningeal spread of high-grade gliomas, are rare pediatric neoplasms with diffuse leptomeningeal dissemination and limited parenchymal involvement. Their often nonspecific neurological symptoms frequently lead to misdiagnosis as infectious or inflammatory meningitis, complicating early identification. METHODS: To investigate these diagnostic challenges, the authors retrospectively reviewed the records of 4 pediatric patients (2-13 years) diagnosed with leptomeningeal glial tumors over a 5-year period. Clinical features, neuroimaging, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) findings, histopathology, treatment, and outcomes were analyzed. RESULTS: All patients had subacute or chronic neurological symptoms, including gait ataxia (4/4), motor weakness (3/4), headache or vomiting (3/4), cognitive or behavioral decline (3/4), and seizures (2/4). Cerebrospinal fluid abnormalities contributed to diagnostic confusion but were not identical across all cases: pleocytosis was present in all patients, elevated protein was documented in those with available protein measurements, and reduced or relatively low glucose was observed only in some patients. Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) demonstrated diffuse leptomeningeal involvement along the neuraxis in all patients, with cystic/nodular T2-hyperintense lesions and variable enhancement. Three patients were diagnosed within the DLGNT/PDLO spectrum and 1 with high-grade glioma with leptomeningeal dissemination. Hydrocephalus requiring ventriculoperitoneal shunting developed in 3/4 patients. All received chemotherapy; 1 required decompressive spinal surgery and craniospinal radiotherapy. At last follow-up, 3 patients were alive with neurological sequelae and 1 had died of progressive disease. CONCLUSION: These findings highlight that pediatric leptomeningeal glial tumors are diagnostically challenging and easily mistaken for chronic meningitis. Characteristic MRI findings, elevated CSF protein, and spinal dissemination are important clues.
Chrysin Ameliorates Seizures, Seizure-induced Oxidative Stress, and Cognitive Impairment in Experimental Models of Epilepsy in Rats.推荐

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Chrysin (CH), a naturally occurring flavonoid found in various traditional medicinal plants and bee products, has been reported to possess antioxidant and neuroprotective properties. These properties may offer therapeutic potential in epilepsy, a chronic neurological disorder frequently accompanied by cognitive impairment and oxidative stress. The aim of the study is to evaluate the anticonvulsant, antioxidative, and cognitive-protective effects of chrysin in experimental models of epilepsy. METHODS: The effects of CH were assessed using three established rodent models: Pentylenetetrazol (PTZ)-induced seizures, Maximal Electroshock (MES)-induced seizures, and PTZ-induced kindling. CONCLUSION: These findings support the anticonvulsant and neuroprotective effects of chrysin and highlight CH as a promising phytochemical candidate for managing epilepsy and its neurocognitive complications.
The Seizure-related Homolog (SEZ6) Family in Cancer: Emerging Biomarkers and Therapeutic Targets.推荐

作者：Rout A, Iqbal H, Rao S, Kumar R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Cancer is a global burden, contributing to about 20 million incident cases and around 10 million mortalities in 2022. Metastasis, or secondary tumor formation, is the prevalent cause of most mortalities. Metastasis and resistance to therapy are major contributors to cancer-related mortality. Expression shifts of cytoplasmic, nuclear, cell-surface, and transmembrane proteins play an important role in tumor progression and therapeutic targeting. This review summarizes the family of SEZ6 proteins, which are single-transmembrane proteins. The family comprises Seizure-related 6 homolog (SEZ6), SEZ6-like (SEZ6L), and SEZ6-like 2 (SEZ6L2) proteins. These proteins are studied for their primary roles in synaptic connectivity, signal transduction, protein interactions, and the mediation of neurodevelopmental and psychiatric disorders. However, the SEZ6 family proteins have also been identified as emerging biomarkers and therapeutic targets across various cancers. Recent studies have shown that SEZ6L2 and SEZ6L, not SEZ6, are involved in complement regulation, which can lead to tumor escape and modulation of the tumor microenvironment. This review summarises the multifaceted roles of SEZ6 family members in modulating the complement system, their expression patterns, prognostic relevance, and the proposed molecular mechanisms underlying tumor aggressiveness, migration, and other malignant phenotypes. This study also discusses the recent advances in SEZ6-targeted ADCs, biomarker-driven patient selection, and future opportunities for cancer biology.
Structure-Activity Relationships and Mechanistic Insights of Racetam Derivatives in Epilepsy Management.推荐
PMID 42973340 · Central nervous system agents in medicinal chemistry · 2026 10 20
作者：Chaudhary T, Dhama N, Sangal S, Kumar A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Epilepsy continues to affect millions globally, and despite advances in treatment, many patients still struggle with drug-resistant forms of the disorder. Among the newer therapeutic options, racetam derivatives have garnered attention for their distinctive mechanism of action-modulating synaptic vesicle protein 2A (SV2A)-which distinguishes them from traditional antiepileptic drugs (AEDs). This review provides a closer examination of the structure-activity relationships (SAR) of racetam analogues, investigating how chemical modifications, such as aromatic substitution, halogenation, and alkyl chain extension, can significantly enhance key properties, including lipophilicity, blood-brain barrier penetration, metabolic stability, and receptor binding. By comparing well-known agents like piracetam and levetiracetam with newer, more refined molecules such as brivaracetam and seletracetam, we highlight the steady evolution in potency, selectivity, and pharmacokinetics. We also explore emerging strategies that aim to address current challenges in drug delivery and tolerability, ranging from stereochemical optimization to nanotechnology-based formulations and prodrug design. Recent findings from both preclinical and clinical studies (2021-2024) underscore the growing relevance of racetams in epilepsy care, highlighting the need for continued innovation in medicinal chemistry and translational research. Ultimately, racetam derivatives represent a compelling intersection of pharmaceutical design and neuropharmacology, offering a promising roadmap for the development of next-generation AEDs tailored to individual patient needs.
Cell-Based Therapeutic Strategies for Correcting Mitochondrial DNA Mutations and Mitigating Oxidative Stress.浏览
PMID 42973330 · Current gene therapy · 2026 10 20
作者：Asif M, Zain M, Manzoor S, Amir M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Mitochondria are essential structures that produce energy in cells through mitochondrial oxidative phosphorylation and are one of the primary sources of reactive oxygen species (ROS). Mutations in mitochondrial DNA (mtDNA) cause a wide range of hereditary illnesses with multisystem clinical symptoms, poor cellular bioenergetics, and decreased mitochondrial oxidative phosphorylation. Effective treatment approaches have been hampered by the genetic complexity associated with mtDNA disorders, including heteroplasmy and tissue-specific diversity. The relationship between mutations in the mitochondrial DNA and oxidative stress is multidimensional and snowballing, with structural malfunction of the electron transport chain encouraging the development of ROS that causes further damage to mitochondrial DNA, lipids, and proteins. Under carefully regulated experimental conditions, cell-based disease models, in particular induced pluripotent stem cells (iPSCs) and standard transmitochondrial cybrids, have become useful tools for studying disease mechanisms, simulating mutation-specific phenotypes, and assessing possible therapeutic interventions. This review provides the key roles of mutations and oxidative stress in the pathogenesis of the disease in the context of the primary role of the mitochondrial genome, its maternal inheritance, and the most common disorders, i.e., mitochondrial encephalomyopathy, lactic acidosis, and stroke-like episodes (MELAS) and myoclonic epilepsy with ragged red fibers (MERRF). We outline the use of cell-based models to study mitochondrial pathologies, including patient-derived primary cells and iPSCs, which could be differentiated into cell types of interest to the disease, and cybrid systems, which could isolate the contribution of single mutations in the mitochondrial DNA to an underlying background of controlled nuclear genetic backgrounds. Besides, the review focuses on innovative technologies to mend mutations in mitochondrial DNA, such as allotopic expression, mitoTALENs and mitoZFNs, and the new base-editing systems like DdCBEs. Included are the free cell-based therapies like mitochondrial transfer and nuclear transfer procedures. Finally, we discussed the measures to be taken to minimize oxidative stress, and these actions are standard: mitochondria- targeted antioxidants, mitophagy, and autophagy regulation. The study also looks at the present level of proof supporting these therapy techniques and discusses the advantages and disadvantages of existing experimental models. Although several gene editing and mitochondria replacement technologies have shown promising results in clinical and cellular research, their clinical translation is still restricted and needs more confirmation in terms of long-term therapeutic efficacy, safety, and delivery efficiency.
Sleep Homeostasis Has Circaseptan and Circatrigintan Cycles Over Year-Long Timescales.推荐
PMID 42973087 · Journal of sleep research · 2026 11 02
作者：Vashin V, Magana-Tellez O, Ahrens E, Mosher JC et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Sleep homeostasis, the process by which sleep pressure accumulated during wakefulness is dissipated across non-rapid eye movement (REM) sleep, can be quantified as the nightly rate of decline in slow-wave activity (SWA; 0.5-4 Hz). Disruption of this homeostatic process has been documented in a range of neurological disorders including epilepsy, Alzheimer's disease and REM sleep behaviour disorder, yet its long-term dynamics in healthy individuals remain uncharacterized. The advent of subscalp EEG (sqEEG) platforms now makes continuous, naturalistic sleep recording feasible over months, enabling longitudinal study of sleep homeostasis at an unprecedented scale. Here we report the first such characterization in 20 healthy adults recorded continuously over 12-15 months. SWA slope was predominantly negative across all subjects, confirming intact homeostatic regulation and was stable across sex and age. Continuous wavelet transform analysis revealed significant circaseptan (~7-day) and circatrigintan (~28-day) rhythmicity in the majority of subjects, independent of sex. These findings establish a normative baseline for long-term sleep homeostatic dynamics and suggest that homeostatic sleep pressure dissipation is organized across multiple timescales beyond the single night, with direct relevance for neurological conditions in which this process is disrupted.
Sellar spine in children and young adults: clinical presentation, imaging characteristics, and pituitary outcomes.浏览
PMID 42973086 · Journal of pediatric endocrinology & metabolism : JPEM · 2026 11 04
作者：Salama MA, Mark IT, Burkett BJ, Lteif AN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: The true clinical relevance of a sellar spine remains unclear because of limited available data. We aimed to describe the presentation and associated pituitary dysfunction among children and young adults with a sellar spine. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed patients with a sellar spine found on brain imaging obtained in individuals less than 25 years of age. Extracted variables included age at initial brain imaging, indication for imaging, and pituitary dysfunction status and type. RESULTS: Eleven patients were included. Age at initial identification of a sellar spine ranged from 6 to 24 years. Indications for neuroimaging included headaches (n=3), seizures (n=2), precocious puberty (n=1), severe obesity with advanced bone age (n=1), suspected stroke/ataxia (n=1), nasal polyp evaluation (n=1), hypogonadotropic hypogonadism and secondary hypothyroidism (n=1), and as part of screening testing for Von Hipple Lindau (n=1). Pituitary stalk deviation was found in 7 patients (63.6 %). Pituitary gland distorted morphology leading to a misdiagnosis of pituitary adenoma found in 2 patients (18.2 %). Among younger children (n=5; age ≤9 years), precocious puberty occurred in one patient who demonstrated pituitary stalk deviation with an AP size-to-sella ratio of 0.5. Another child with stalk deviation did not develop precocious puberty. Pituitary hormone deficiency was identified in one patient (hypogonadotropic hypogonadism and hypothyroidism) with stalk deviation and AP size-to-sella ratio of 0.63. CONCLUSIONS: The presence of a sellar spine can distort pituitary anatomy and result in diagnostic misclassification. Endocrinopathies may develop in some affected patients. Further, larger studies are warranted to better define the characteristics of sellar spine leading to endocrinopathies.
Disentangling the Genetic Relationship Between Sleep and Epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42973051 · Annals of clinical and translational neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Le KN, Jackson VE, Stirling RE, Gerring ZF et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: A bidirectional relationship between sleep and epilepsy is well recognised, yet the biological mechanisms linking them remain poorly understood. Both have a genetic basis, with genome-wide association studies (GWAS) capturing polygenic contributions. By leveraging GWAS summary statistics, we aimed to investigate their shared genetic architecture. METHODS: We analysed seven sleep traits (morningness chronotype, sleep duration, habitual short/long sleep, insomnia, daytime napping/sleepiness) with common epilepsy types (focal epilepsy and genetic generalised epilepsy [GGE]). For each trait pair, we estimated genome-wide genetic correlation, tested bidirectional causality, quantified polygenic overlap, performed gene-trait associations to identify putative pleiotropic genes, estimated local genetic correlations and investigated whether regions of significance were driven by shared causal variants using colocalisation and fine-mapping. RESULTS: Genome-wide analyses supported a positive genetic correlation between higher GGE risk and habitual short sleep, with extensive polygenic overlap (75%-97%) across seven sleep traits. Gene-trait analyses identified RMI1 as a putative sleep-epilepsy candidate, with 13 additional sleep-associated genes linked to monogenic epilepsies (e.g., KANSL1, GRIN2A). Locus-specific analyses identified 16 genomic loci of interest (13 sleep-GGE, 3 sleep-focal epilepsy). Morningness-GGE shared the largest number of locus-specific associations. No significant causal relationships were identified, and causal variants did not colocalise. INTERPRETATION: Our findings support complex shared genetic contributions between sleep and epilepsy, with distinct patterns for focal compared to generalised epilepsy. These differences may help explain syndrome-specific sleep-wake associations, including seizure timing and predisposition. By localising shared loci and highlighting specific overlapping genes, we provide a foundation for future research into the mechanisms underlying epilepsy-sleep comorbidities.
Optogenetic Stimulation of Insular Parvalbumin-Positive Interneurons Reveals CA3 Resonant Properties and Rebound Seizure Susceptibility.推荐
PMID 42973041 · Journal of neurophysiology · 2026 11 02
作者：Lévesque M, Avoli M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Seizures in mesial temporal lobe epilepsy (MTLE) can spread to the insular cortex (IC) due to its connections with hippocampal and para-hippocampal areas. The contribution of specific cell types in the IC to ictogenesis remains however unclear. Therefore, we analyzed in the pilocarpine model of MTLE the effects of optogenetic activation of IC parvalbumin (PV)-positive interneurons on CA3 field activity. Light pulses were delivered at 0.5 Hz (300 s ON, 600 s OFF) in IC, between day 4 and 14 after status epilepticus in PV-ChR2 mice. During interictal periods, this procedure evoked CA3 field responses characterized by 5 Hz oscillations, which likely mirrored heightened inhibitory activity as suggested by a shift toward higher theta/beta ratio and a more negative spectral exponent. Analysis of ictal activity revealed that seizures occurred at higher rates during days of stimulation (day 4-14) compared to days with no stimulation (day 15-24) (p < 0.05). During the 300 s optogenetic stimulation cycle, seizures occurred at high rates between 301 and 502 s after the end of stimulation (p < 0.05). Our findings suggest that during the interictal phase, the synchronized activation of PV-positive interneurons in the IC imposes a GABAergic drive that, through post-inhibitory rebound mechanisms, entrains the CA3 network into a 5 Hz resonant oscillation. During states of pathological hyperexcitability, this synchronized rebound activity reaches a threshold that converts a rhythmic oscillation into a seizure. Stimulation of the IC could bias the hippocampal network toward 5 Hz oscillatory states and promote ictogenesis via inhibitory-rebound synchronization.
Early milestones and neurodevelopment in children with mitochondrial disease.推荐
PMID 42972980 · Developmental medicine and child neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Gordon-Lipkin EM, Wood M, Hesselgesser M, Farmer C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：AIM: To quantify neurodevelopmental functioning in children with primary mitochondrial disease (MtD), and to assess relationships with other clinical data. METHOD: This study was performed as a cross-sectional analysis of a longitudinal cohort. Inclusion criteria were age over 12 months, genetically confirmed MtD. Clinical data were obtained by caregiver interview and medical records abstraction. Measures included IQ, Vineland Adaptive Behavior Scales, Third Edition, the International Pediatric Mitochondrial Disease Scale (IPMDS), and Gross Motor Functional Classification System (GMFCS) level. An exploratory approach analyzed the relationship between clinical data and other measures, using Vineland Adaptive Behavior Scales, Third Edition as a dependent variable. RESULTS: The cohort included 32 children, mean age 7 years (SD = 3 years 6 months). The most common genotypes included MT-ATP6 (n = 6) and the mitochondrial aminoacyl tRNA synthetase family of genes. Regarding phenotype, 15 out of 32 (47%) met clinical criteria for Leigh syndrome. Clinical descriptors of disability included 18 out of 32 (56%) with intellectual disability/global developmental delay; 13 out of 32 (40%) were non-ambulatory and 6 out of 32 (19%) reported developmental regression, primarily in the motor domain. The mean Full Scale IQ was 54 (SD = 27) and adaptive behavior composite (ABC) standard score was 66 (SD = 20) with motor subscale lower than other domains. The ABC was negatively associated with IPMDS, GMFCS, and seizures. Of those who walked and talked by 24 months and had neuroimaging with zero or one brain region affected (n = 9), eight (89%) had adaptive function in the average range. INTERPRETATION: Children with MtD exhibit a wide range of neurodevelopmental function. Overall MtD adaptive function was low, with motor-predominant disability, and correlated negatively with other measures of severity. Episodic developmental regression occurred in a minority of this cohort but primarily affected motor skills.
Enantioselective Analysis of Ketamine and Other Substances Commonly Found in "Pink Cocaine" Preparations via Capillary Electrophoresis and Liquid Chromatography With Ultraviolet Detection.浏览
PMID 42972946 · Drug testing and analysis · 2026 11 02
作者：Sartor L, Schulze L, Greif M, Martin N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Rising seizures of illicit ketamine-in pure crystalline form and in complex preparations such as pink cocaine-have heightened interest in tracing its origin. As many psychoactive drugs are chiral, knowledge of their enantiomeric composition in seized samples can support source attribution and legal assessment under drug control laws. Enantioselective methods were optimized for five chiral substances commonly found in pink cocaine, namely ketamine, amphetamine, MDMA, tramadol, and mephedrone; the achiral substances 2C-B, lidocaine, and caffeine were also included. Capillary electrophoresis (CE) was performed using sulfated β-cyclodextrin as chiral selector in the run buffer. A CE-UV short-end injection method enabled enantioselective analysis of ketamine in crystalline samples within 4 min (resolution: 6). A CE-UV long-end injection method enabled enantioselective separation of complex mixtures within 17 min (resolution: 2.7-11.1). For high-performance liquid chromatography (HPLC)-UV, a vancomycin-based chiral stationary phase provided resolutions of 1.2-4.7 within 27 min for pink cocaine samples. CE analysis demonstrated superior enantioselectivity, separation efficiency, and shorter analysis times compared to HPLC. However, CE was less robust due to peak area variability and batch-to-batch variation of the sulfated β-cyclodextrin, whereas HPLC allows for reliable quantitation. Of 558 seized ketamine samples analyzed, all but one were racemic, suggesting that (S)-ketamine is currently used mainly in legal pharmaceutical products and is not diverted to the illicit market as large-scale drug substance batches. Nevertheless, chiral analysis should be applied continuously to monitor the market, as the availability of enantiomerically pure drug preparations could lead to future market changes.
