The therapeutic landscape of cannabidiol: a systematic review of molecular mechanisms, clinical efficacy in oncology and neurology, and regulatory changes.浏览
PMID 42972463 · Journal of cannabis research · 2026 08 06
作者：Trif C, Pechyen C, Parcharoen Y
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Despite extensive research on CBD, a comprehensive analysis of its rapidly evolving molecular understanding, clinical validation across diverse fields such as oncology and neurology, safety concerns, and dynamic global regulatory changes remain fragmented. This review addresses a critical translation gap by integrating quantitative molecular polypharmacology with the most recent global regulatory thresholds. Furthermore, it evaluates how pharmacogenomic modeling and advanced nanocarrier delivery systems based on artificial intelligence can actively resolve the pharmacokinetic limitations of CBD and dose-dependent hepatotoxicity, establishing a clear, data-driven design for personalized cannabinoid medicine. CBD acts as a broad-spectrum modulator through multiple molecular targets, including cannabinoid and non-cannabinoid receptors, contributing to its pharmacological effects. Clinical evidence strongly supports its efficacy in pediatric seizure disorders and symptom management in oncology, while topical applications show promise in pain and dermatological conditions. However, systemic CBD use for chronic pain, psychiatric disorders, and as a primary anti-tumor agent still requires reliable clinical validation. Regulatory frameworks are changing globally in response to emerging safety concerns, particularly hepatotoxicity and drug interactions mediated by CYP450 inhibition. Future research priorities should include large-scale trials to clarify the inter-entourage effect, long-term safety assessments, metabolic interventions and precision delivery systems. This review highlights the necessity of evidence-based approaches to unlock the full clinical potential of CBD within a controlled, scientific-based approach.
Clinical, neuroimaging, and genetic analyses of isolated sulfite oxidase deficiency in Chinese neonates: a retrospective case series with a narrative synthesis of previously reported cases.推荐
PMID 42972410 · Italian journal of pediatrics · 2026 07 31
作者：Zhang W, Yang H, Xu L, Liu Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Currently, no curative treatment is available for isolated sulfite oxidase deficiency (ISOD), and disease progression is primarily managed by restricting sulfur-containing amino acids in the diet. Furthermore, despite its overall rarity, ISOD prevalence in the Chinese population has demonstrated an upward trend in recent years. Accordingly, this study investigated the clinical phenotype, imaging characteristics, mutation spectrum, and prognosis of ISOD in Chinese newborns. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed the clinical data from seven neonates with genetically confirmed ISOD from southeastern China between November 2018 and March 2025. including maternal and infant factors, clinical manifestations, laboratory results, and neuroimaging and genetic findings. Given the rarity of ISOD and the limited number of cases, we also performed a narrative synthesis of previously reported Chinese neonatal ISOD cases (n = 13) identified through a structured literature search to provide additional clinical context. The retrospective case series and previously published cases are presented descriptively, resulting in a total cohort of 20 patients. RESULTS: All infants (20/20,100%) presented with intractable seizures within the first few days of life. Other common clinical presentations included increased muscle tone (15/20,75%) and feeding difficulty (14/20,70%). Lens dislocation was uncommon in this neonatal cohort (1/20,5%). Cranial neuroimaging abnormalities, predominantly symmetric abnormal signals involving bilateral cerebral hemispheres and basal ganglia, were detected in all 19 patients who completed brain imaging examinations; these characteristic radiological manifestations are easily misdiagnosed as hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy. Laboratory tests revealed elevated urinary sulfite levels or decreased plasma homocysteine levels in some cases. Genetic analysis identified 11 distinct pathogenic variants of the SUOX gene, with c.1200 C > G (p.Tyr400Ter) being the most common mutation in China. The prognosis was extremely poor; only five patients (5/20,25%) survived following symptomatic treatment, including sulfur-restricted diets, with a median follow-up of 10 months (range: 6-18 months). All surviving infants exhibited refractory epilepsy and severe developmental delays. CONCLUSION: The hallmark features of ISOD in Chinese neonates include refractory seizures occurring shortly after birth and characteristic neuroimaging patterns. The c.1200 C > G (p.Tyr400Ter) SUOX gene mutation is the most common pathogenic variant. Given the poor prognosis and lack of effective treatment, prenatal genetic diagnosis is recommended for early intervention in high-risk families.
Structural features and disease-associated functions of ADGRV1: insights from signaling mechanisms.推荐
PMID 42972392 · Cell communication and signaling : CCS · 2026 08 12
作者：Liu Q, Tian J, Xin L, Liu F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Adhesion G protein-coupled receptor V1 (ADGRV1) is one of the largest and most modular receptors in the adhesion G protein-coupled receptor (aGPCR) family, integrating functions of cell adhesion, mechanosensing, and classical G protein signaling. Its structural domains, including calcium exchanger β (Calx-β) repeats, laminin G-like/ pentraxin (LamG/PTX) domain, epilepsy-associated (EPTP/EAR) repeats, GPCR-autoproteolysis-inducing (GAIN) domain/GPCR proteolytic site (GPS) module with tethered Stachel peptide, seven transmembrane (7TM) helices, and PDZ-binding motif (PBM), jointly shape its activation, intracellular transport, and effector coupling. Recent studies indicate that ADGRV1 exhibits context-dependent signaling, particularly through G protein coupling and ciliary localization, thereby influencing calcium homeostasis, autophagy, and developmental pathways. Genetic and functional evidence links ADGRV1 to Usher syndrome (USH) type II, epilepsy, and emerging associations with Alzheimer's disease (AD), and recurrent somatic alterations in diverse cancers. Variants in ADGRV1 modulate processes such as ankle-link complex (ALC) assembly, synaptic stability, amyloid and tau regulation, chemoresistance, and immune microenvironment remodeling. Despite this progress, many aspects of its signaling mechanisms, ligand interactions, and disease relevance remain poorly understood. Future work combining high-resolution structural studies, systematic mapping of signaling bias, and rigorous translational validation will be essential to clarify the multifaceted roles of ADGRV1 and to evaluate its potential as a biomarker and therapeutic target.
KARS1-Related Infantile-Onset Progressive Leukoencephalopathy With or Without Deafness: A Novel Variant and Phenotypic Expansion.推荐
PMID 42972158 · Journal of molecular neuroscience : MN · 2026 11 03
作者：Behroozi J, Khorrami M, Farhadi A, Ghazavi A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Biallelic variants in the KARS1 gene, encoding lysyl-tRNA synthetase, are responsible for autosomal recessive disorders such as infantile-onset progressive leukoencephalopathy with or without deafness (LEPID; OMIM #619147) and nonsyndromic hearing loss (DFNB89). Common features of LEPID include sensorineural hearing loss, developmental delay, and leukoencephalopathy, but dysmorphic facial features and cardiac anomalies are not commonly reported. This study presents the first documented LEPID case in Iran, expanding the phenotypic and genetic spectrum of KARS1-related disorders. Whole exome sequencing (WES) was performed on a two-year-old Iranian boy from non-consanguineous parents, without a positive history of any neurologic conditions. Identified variants were validated by Sanger sequencing, with familial segregation analysis. Moreover, a literature review of 41 KARS1-related cases was conducted to compare clinical and genetic findings. WES identified two compound heterozygous KARS1 variants (NM_005548.3): c.959G > A (p.Arg320His) and c.2T > C (p.Met1Thr), previously reported and novel, respectively, both of which were classified as likely pathogenic according to the American College of Medical Genetics and Genomics criteria. Literature review confirmed these dysmorphic features as novel, absent in prior cases, with cardiac involvement in a limited number of patients. This study highlights a novel KARS1 variant and an expanded phenotypic spectrum of LEPID, emphasizing the role of WES in diagnosing rare disorders even in non-consanguineous marriages. These findings advocate for broader phenotypic recognition and functional studies to elucidate KARS1's role in neurodevelopmental, craniofacial, and cardiac abnormalities.
Antiseizure medication-induced changes in serum ephrin-B2 and their association with treatment response in adults with generalised seizure disorders in Northern India: protocol for a prospective observational cohort study.推荐

作者：Bankar M, Verma A, Gupta A

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Epilepsy affects approximately 65 million people worldwide, and India carries close to one-fifth of the global burden. Although generalised seizures account for a substantial share of new epilepsy diagnoses, approximately one-third of patients do not respond to their first antiseizure medication (ASM). Currently, there is a critical lack of reliable blood biomarkers to predict these treatment outcomes. Ephrin-B2 (encoded by EFNB2) regulates neuronal excitability, neurogenesis and synaptic plasticity via the mTOR and MAPK pathways; Mendelian randomisation evidence indicates that lower serum ephrin-B2 is associated with increased epilepsy risk. Whether ASM therapy raises serum ephrin-B2 and whether this tracks therapeutic response is unknown. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: This single-centre, prospective, observational cohort study will enrol 50 adults (18–65 years) with newly diagnosed generalised seizures initiating valproate or levetiracetam monotherapy at clinician discretion. Serum ephrin-B2 will be measured by sandwich enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) at baseline and 24 weeks. The primary outcome is the within-participant change in serum ephrin-B2 to week 24; secondary outcomes are its correlation with the percentage reduction in 28-day seizure frequency and the between-drug difference in ephrin-B2 change. Responders are predefined as achieving ≥50% seizure reduction. Analyses comprise paired t-tests or Wilcoxon signed-rank tests, Pearson/Spearman correlation with regression and receiver operating characteristic analysis of ephrin-B2 as a response biomarker. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: The study has been approved by the Institutional Ethics Committee, AIIMS Raebareli (IEC 2025-5 IMP-11) and follows ICMR National Ethical Guidelines for Biomedical and Health Research (2017) with written informed consent from all participants. Findings will be disseminated through peer-reviewed publication and conferences.
Artificial Intelligence for developing a personalised functional profile and supporting upper-limb rehabilitation decision-making in children with unilateral Cerebral Palsy (AInCP): protocol for an observational study.浏览
PMID 42971618 · BMJ open · 2026 11 02
作者：Sgandurra G, Kalkantzi A, Beani E, Cioni G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Cerebral palsy (CP) is a lifelong non-progressive disorder, resulting from disturbances in the developing or infant brain, with approximately 17 million people worldwide being diagnosed. More than one-third of the population with CP has unilateral CP (UCP), whereby the primary sensorimotor impairments affect predominantly one side of the body, mainly the upper limb (UpL), compromising independence and quality of life. Associated comorbidities (eg, cognitive, visual, behavioural impairments and epilepsy) can impede the establishment of a clear functional diagnosis, consequently compromising the formation of effective intervention strategies. Driven by the clinical presentation complexity of UCP, the main objective of the Artificial Intelligence in Cerebral Palsy (AInCP) study is to establish a valid functional diagnosis supporting accurate rehabilitative priorities, identifying multidimensional functional profiles that may inform personalised rehabilitation planning, utilising AI. Through the integration of multimodal data, the ultimate goal is to develop and test evidence-based clinical decision support tools for the personalised functional diagnosis and UpL assessment in children with UCP by developing, testing and validating AI-cost-effective strategies. This will enable more accurate, personalised functional diagnoses and treatment planning, enhancing quality of life and efficiency of care for children with UCP. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: This prospective, multicentre, observational clinical study will include 250 children with UCP and 250 typically developing children (TDC). Participants will be enrolled and assessed within four clinical centres distributed across Europe. Data from children with UCP will be collected by three broad domains: clinical assessments and questionnaires, neuroimaging data and data from UpL daily-life monitoring using wearable sensors. TDC will be evaluated using clinical tests and wearable sensors. All the items identified and the whole methodology have been co-created and co-designed among a multidisciplinary team, including caregivers and persons with lived experience. Data will be analysed with AI and machine-learning algorithms to understand the interdependencies across functional domains and create functional profiles that can guide personalised treatment planning. Non-clinical data such as sleep quality, technology familiarity and technology acceptance will also be collected to provide an empirical foundation for conducting an early health technology assessment of the solutions developed within the AInCP project. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: Ethical approval has been obtained by all involved clinical sites. Written informed consent will be obtained from the legal representatives of all participating children, and age-appropriate assent will be obtained from children according to local ethical requirements. All procedures will be closely monitored by ethicists during the implementation of AI to ensure patient privacy, prevent bias and avoid unintended harm while developing robust, fair and trustworthy AI solutions. The results of this study will be disseminated through open-access peer-reviewed journals relevant for paediatrics to ensure wide accessibility. Results will be communicated by the professionals directly involved in the clinical study through national and international academic conferences, relevant associations and organisations, including policy makers. TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT06073522.
Perceptions of living with drug-resistant epilepsy and the potential role of neuropalliative care.推荐
PMID 42971489 · Journal of pain and symptom management · 2026 11 02
作者：Hixon AM, Drees C, Lopez-Esquibel N, Fetrow K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: To assess quality of life factors in persons with drug-resistant epilepsy (PWDRE) and determine if PWDRE would be interested in palliative/neuropalliative care involvement. METHODS: For this qualitative study, adults living with DRE (n=20) participated in semi-structured interviews regarding personal identity, relationships, medical care, and needs related to living with epilepsy. Participants were introduced to the term "palliative care" (PC) and queried on its potential usefulness within their regular medical care. Responses were coded thematically. Clinical data were extracted from medical records. RESULTS: Most participants felt DRE had significantly affected their perception of self, family, and social life. CONCLUSIONS: Many PWDRE expressed an interest in the potential benefit of palliative care involvement in addition to their routine medical care. Endoscopic Hematoma Evacuation and Controlled Inner Membranectomy Using a Modified Single Burr-Hole Technique Under Local Anesthesia for Septated Chronic Subdural Hematomas in Elderly Patients: A Multicenter Retrospective Study.浏览

摘要：OBJECTIVE: Septated chronic subdural hematoma (sCSDH) in elderly patients may contain organized components that limit conventional burr-hole drainage. This study evaluates the technical feasibility, perioperative course, and early clinical and radiological outcomes of controlled partial inner membranecotomy using modified single-port endoscopic drainage under local anesthesia in selected elderly patients with sCSDH. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed patients aged 65 and older who underwent endoscopic drainage of MRI-confirmed sCSDH under local anesthesia at two neurosurgical centers between March 2022 and July 2025. Demographic characteristics, anesthesia records, complications, radiological findings, and early clinical outcomes were evaluated. RESULTS: Eleven of 29 patients (38%) received antiplatelet or anticoagulant therapy. Mean hematoma thickness was 21 ± 6 mm, preoperative midline shift was 10 ± 4 mm, and operative time was 47 ± 10 minutes. At postoperative one month, 26 patients (90%) had an mRS score of 0-1, compared with 9 (31%) preoperatively. Recurrence occurred in 3 patients (10%); 1 (3%) required reoperation, while 2 (7%) were treated conservatively. Postoperative seizures occurred in 2 patients (7%). No patient required conversion to general anesthesia, and no perioperative deaths, surgical site infections, or permanent neurological deficits were observed. CONCLUSION: Endoscopic hematoma evacuation and controlled partial membranecotomy via a single burr hole under local anesthesia and sedation were technically feasible in selected patients and associated with acceptable short-term outcomes. Without a control group, superiority over other surgical approaches or an independent benefit of inner membranotomy cannot be demonstrated.
Beyond classification accuracy: Building trustworthy artificial intelligence for clinical electroencephalography.推荐
PMID 42971410 · Brain research bulletin · 2026 11 02
作者：Hu S, Huang Y, Zhang J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Electroencephalography (EEG) remains important in clinical neuroscience because it records brain activity as a time-resolved physiological process. It offers millisecond temporal resolution, can be used at the bedside and supports prolonged monitoring. Artificial intelligence (AI) has expanded EEG analysis through automated pattern recognition, representation learning, large-scale screening and decision support in epilepsy, sleep medicine, cognitive disorders, psychiatric conditions, intensive care, disorders of consciousness and anaesthesia monitoring. High classification accuracy in a research dataset, however, is not clinical readiness. EEG signals are shaped by physiological state, artefacts, acquisition systems, preprocessing choices and clinical context. Labels may come from expert annotations, diagnostic categories, behavioural scales, treatment responses or long-term outcomes, and these sources do not always define the same biological target. This review argues that EEG-AI translation should be judged by the strength of its evidence, not by technical performance alone. We examine why EEG is valuable yet hard to standardize, how EEG features can serve as candidate biomarkers, and why translational maturity differs across clinical scenarios. We then discuss the evidence gaps behind high-performing models, focusing on target validity, data leakage, confounding, external validation and clinically aligned metrics. Finally, we argue for clinical understandability rather than model interpretability alone, and for human-machine workflows rather than isolated classifiers. Future EEG-AI systems should be assessed by whether their outputs are biologically plausible, externally generalizable, understandable to clinicians and safe in real-world care.
Plasma cytokines associated with anxiety comorbidity in adult epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42971409 · Brain research bulletin · 2026 11 02
作者：Wang K, Li L, Yang Y, Li Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Anxiety comorbidity in epilepsy may reflect altered immune-brain communication, but the peripheral cytokine profile associated with this phenotype remains insufficiently characterized. We examined plasma cytokine alterations in adult epilepsy and explored whether immune-clinical modelling could identify a stable comorbidity-related signature. METHODS: We retrospectively enrolled 124 adults with epilepsy, including 35 with anxiety comorbidity and 89 without. Plasma concentrations of 12 cytokines (IFN-α, IFN-γ, TNF-α, IL-1β, IL-2, IL-4, IL-5, IL-6, IL-8, IL-10, IL-12p70, and IL-17A) and routine hematological parameters were evaluated. Cytokines were measured using a multiplex bead-based flow fluorescent immunoassay. Group comparisons, univariable logistic regression, LASSO regression with bootstrap stability selection, and multivariable logistic regression were performed. Model performance was assessed as an exploratory integrative framework. RESULTS: Patients with anxiety comorbidity had significantly lower plasma IFN-α levels than those without anxiety comorbidity (P = 0.001). In the final four-variable model, lower IFN-α (adjusted OR 0.20, 95% CI 0.05-0.67; P = 0.009) and lower mean corpuscular hemoglobin (adjusted OR 0.79, 95% CI 0.65-0.94; P = 0.009) were independently associated with anxiety comorbidity. IL-4 showed a positive association, whereas IL-17A showed an inverse association. The model showed moderate discrimination and acceptable calibration. CONCLUSIONS: Anxiety comorbidity in adult epilepsy is associated with peripheral cytokine alterations, particularly reduced plasma IFN-α. These findings suggest type I interferon-related immune dysregulation within a broader peripheral immune-network imbalance. Prospective validation and mechanistic studies are warranted.
Development and validation of a multimodal data-based scale for predicting epilepsy surgery outcomes.推荐
PMID 42970886 · Epilepsy research · 2026 10 31
作者：Yuan C, Huang F, Sun Y, Ge S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Seizure outcomes after drug-resistant epilepsy surgery are hard to predict, and existing models lack rigorous validation. We developed and internally validated a multimodal scale predicting seizure freedom. METHODS: Retrospectively, 157 of 816 screened patients with complete data and ≥ 1 year follow-up were analyzed (116 seizure-free [SF], 41 non-seizure-free [NSF]). Predictors came from clinical variables, MRI, FDG-PET, video-EEG, and MEG. From 56 variables, eight predictors were fixed by screening, clinical judgment, four machine-learning algorithms, and minimum-AIC logistic regression, then re-estimated in all 157 patients by Firth's bias-corrected logistic regression without repeat selection. Internal validation used 2000 bootstrap resamples. A cutoff maximizing 0.4 ×sensitivity + 0.6 ×specificity was selected once in the full cohort and applied unchanged to the ≥ 24 months subgroup. RESULTS: The model retained disease duration, age at surgery, four EEG patterns, temporal MRI lesion, and unilateral unifocal PET hypometabolism. Development-phase AUCs (0.864 training, 0.810 test) were descriptive. Firth re-estimation gave an apparent AUC of 0.771 and an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.724 (Brier 0.156/0.176). At the 79.64-point cutoff, sensitivity was 61.2% and specificity 85.4%; all 95% confidence intervals included the null. Among 101 patients followed ≥ 24 months, the locked model gave AUC 0.770 (95% CI 0.664-0.865), Brier 0.158, sensitivity 53.9%, specificity 96.0%. CONCLUSIONS: Multimodal data integration yielded a fully specified, reproducible eight-variable scale with stable discrimination in longer-term follow-up, supporting exploratory use for preoperative risk stratification and counseling alongside presurgical evaluation. Prospective multicenter validation is the next step toward clinical implementation.

Germline Genetic Variants and Epilepsy Surgery Response: Individual Participant Pooled Analysis of 272 Patients.推荐
PMID 42970749 · Neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Ivaniuk A, Bajaj S, Boßelmann CM, Koh HY et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Genetic testing is increasingly used in presurgical evaluation, but the yield of resection and ablation across germline genetic epilepsies remains uncertain. METHODS: We conducted a systematic review of MEDLINE (PubMed) and Scopus and added cases from 3 institutional cohorts and the Pediatric Epilepsy Research Consortium (PERC) databases, including individuals with pathogenic/likely pathogenic germline variants besides tuberous sclerosis who underwent resection or laser ablation. Etiologies were grouped into biologically informed categories (GATORopathies, vascular, overgrowth, copy number variants (CNVs), channelopathies, synaptopathies, and other). Primary outcome was seizure freedom (Engel I) at the last follow-up. Group comparisons used Fisher exact and Kruskal-Wallis tests (α = 0.05; Bonferroni when applicable). Prespecified sensitivity analyses stratified by lesional status, excluded GATORopathies, and restricted to literature-only cases. RESULTS: We included 224 literature cases (65 studies), 36 institutional cases, and 12 PERC cases (n = 272). The median follow-up was 24 months (interquartile range 12-48.0). Seizure freedom was achieved in vascular disorders 14/19 (73.6%), GATORopathies 79/120 (65.8%), CNVs 18/31 (58.0%), overgrowth 9/16 (56.6%), channelopathies 13/43 (30.0%), synaptopathies 4/18 (22.2%), and other 11/25 (44.0%) (overall p < 0.001). Among cases with available imaging results, 208/253 (82.2%) had epileptogenic MRI lesions, including 64% of channelopathies and 80% of the synaptopathies. In univariate contrasts (each category vs all others), odds of seizure freedom were higher for vascular disorders (2.64-fold, 95% CI 0.87-9.65) and GATORopathies (2.41-fold, 95% CI 1.44-4.09), and lower for synaptopathies (∼4.2-fold lower, OR 0.23, 95% CI 0.05-0.77) and channelopathies (∼4.5-fold lower, OR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09-0.48). Direction and magnitude were consistent across prespecified sensitivity analyses (lesional only, literature only, exclusion of GATORopathies). DISCUSSION: Resective surgeries can be effective in germline genetic epilepsies, but outcomes vary by pathway. Disorders with discrete, lesional substrates (GATORopathies, vascular) show the highest likelihood of seizure freedom, whereas channelopathies and synaptopathies, despite the presence of MRI lesions, have substantially lower yields even in lesional cases. Prospective, genotype-aware surgical registries with standardized reporting (EEG, imaging, and pathology) and time-to-event outcomes are needed to refine the selection of surgical candidates and quantify seizure and non-seizure-related outcomes.
Carbapenem-valproic acid derivative drug-drug interactions: Evaluation of a pharmacist-facing OurPractice Advisory.浏览
PMID 42970581 · American journal of health-system pharmacy : AJHP : official journal of the American Society of Health-System Pharmacists · 2026 11 02
作者：Schuchter K, Layton A, May CC
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: The purpose of this quality improvement evaluation was to describe implementation and postimplementation experience with a pharmacist-facing OurPractice Advisory (OPA) within the Epic electronic health record designed to identify concurrent carbapenem antibiotic and valproic acid (VPA) derivative therapy. SUMMARY: A pharmacist-facing OPA was developed to identify selected clinical scenarios involving carbapenem antibiotics and VPA derivatives, including scenarios involving active inpatient medication orders and prior-to-admission medication list entries. A retrospective review was conducted for hospitalized patients with OPA activations over a 10-month period. The OPA appeared for 30 hospitalized patients. The most common activation scenario was a carbapenem ordered in the presence of an already verified inpatient VPA derivative order, occurring in 19 patients (63.3%). The most common VPA derivative indication was mood stabilization in 12 patients (40%), followed by a seizure-related indication in 9 patients (30%). The median (IQR) time to pharmacist order decision was 21.5 (5.25-51.25) minutes. Formal infectious diseases consultation and neurology consultation occurred in 23 (76.7%) and 10 (33.3%) patients, respectively. CONCLUSION: Implementation of a pharmacist-facing OPA provided a structured process for identifying and documenting selected carbapenem-VPA derivative drug-drug interaction scenarios during pharmacist order verification. Postimplementation review identified opportunities for refinement, including reduction of repetitive activations, clarification of acknowledgment options, and consideration of earlier prescriber-facing clinical decision support.
Global Epidemiology of Neonatal and Infantile-Onset Epilepsies and Epilepsy Syndromes (GENIE): A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.推荐
PMID 42970304 · JAMA neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Safeer V S M, Singh A, Nahan N, Madaan P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：IMPORTANCE: Despite the profound impact of neonatal and infantile-onset epilepsies on neurodevelopmental outcomes and mortality, consolidated global epidemiological evidence remains lacking. OBJECTIVES: To estimate the global incidence and prevalence of neonatal and infantile-onset epilepsies and epilepsy syndromes and to characterize their associated etiological profiles. DATA SOURCES: PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, and Scopus were searched from inception through December 18, 2025. STUDY SELECTION: Population-based observational studies reporting the incidence or prevalence of neonatal and infantile-onset epilepsies or epilepsy syndromes were eligible for inclusion. DATA EXTRACTION AND SYNTHESIS: Two authors independently and in duplicate performed study selection, data extraction, and quality assessment of included studies using the Joanna Briggs Institute Critical Appraisal checklist for prevalence studies. Generalized linear mixed-model random-effects meta-analysis was used to pool the estimates of incidence, prevalence, and etiology. Subgroup meta-analyses were conducted by country, World Health Organization (WHO) region, World Bank income, and diagnostic criteria. Metaregression was used to investigate the association between study-level covariates and pooled incidence estimates of infantile-onset epilepsy syndromes. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Cumulative incidence and prevalence of neonatal and infantile-onset epilepsies and epilepsy syndromes. RESULTS: A total of 86 population-based studies from 30 countries across 4 WHO regions were included. The pooled cumulative incidence of infantile-onset epilepsies within the first 2 years of life was 236.0 cases (95% CI, 164.7-338.2; 95% prediction interval [PI], 53.0-1044.3) per 100 000 live births. Among epilepsy syndrome subgroups, developmental and epileptic encephalopathies (DEEs) had the highest incidence at 67.7 cases (95% CI, 42.1-108.9; 95% PI, 7.1-636.1) per 100 000 live births. Infantile epileptic spasms syndrome (IESS) was the most incident DEE, with 31.6 cases (95% CI, 27.5-36.2; 95% PI, 13.7-72.6) per 100 000 live births. Among self-limited epilepsies, self-limited infantile epilepsy had the highest incidence at 13.0 cases (95% CI, 9.2-18.2; 95% PI, 6.4-26.4) per 100 000 live births. Subgroup analyses revealed limited geographical representativeness, with the majority of studies from high-income countries in the European Region. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: The findings underscore a significant global burden of neonatal and infantile-onset epilepsies, primarily driven by DEEs, specifically IESS. Given substantial between-study heterogeneity, the pooled estimates should be interpreted as broad indicators rather than precise, generalizable rates. Geographic disparities, with a lack of data from the African and Eastern Mediterranean Regions, underscore the need for population-based studies in underrepresented regions and longitudinal research on survival, remission, and functional outcomes.
More than seizures: sensory processing and modulation in pediatric epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42970208 · Acta neurologica Belgica · 2026 11 02
作者：Taş SA, Göksülük MB, Özcan ÖK, Yeşilyurt SY et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy is one of the most common chronic neurological disorders in childhood and is frequently associated with motor, sensory, cognitive, and emotional difficulties. Sensory modulation and sensory processing difficulties are well described in neurodevelopmental conditions such as autism spectrum disorder; similar difficulties, emerging evidence suggests, may also be present in children without neurodevelopmental comorbidities, indicating the potential for mechanisms that operate independently within the context of epilepsy. METHODS: The purpose of this study was to evaluate sensory processing and modulation characteristics in children aged three to ten years by comparing a group of children with epilepsy to typically developing peers. Sensory processing and modulation were assessed using the Sensory Profile, a standardized caregiver-reported questionnaire. RESULTS: Visual processing was the only subscale showing a between-group difference, with lower scores in children with epilepsy than in the control group (p = 0.042, r = 0.282). However, this difference was not retained after adjustment for caregiver educational level and place of residence and was not reflected in the age-referenced performance classifications. CONCLUSION: The sensory processing and modulation profiles of children with epilepsy were largely comparable to those of their typically developing peers. As the difference in visual processing scores was not supported by the adjusted analysis or age-referenced classifications, it should be regarded as preliminary and interpreted cautiously; larger studies with more balanced caregiver characteristics are needed to assess its reproducibility, clinical relevance, and the contribution of epilepsy-related clinical variables.

Assessing the "epileptic personality".推荐
PMID 42970084 · History of psychology · 2026 11 02
作者：Gyimesi J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：This article traces the historical development, conceptualization, and assessment of the theory of the epileptic personality, with particular attention to the contributions of Hermann Rorschach, Françoise Minkowska, and Leopold Szondi. The theory of the epileptic personality characterized individuals with epilepsy by heightened emotionality, difficulties in social adaptation, egocentricity, sexual deviance, increased aggression and violence, and, in many cases, pronounced susceptibility to religious experience. As a rich and long-standing component of 19th-century medical thought, the theory of the epileptic personality continued to exert influence well into the 20th century, including its impact on the development of personality assessment tools. Rorschach and Szondi explicitly assessed the notion of the epileptic personality through their respective tests. Subsequent research has, however, demonstrated that neither instrument measures a personality uniquely linked to epilepsy; rather, both capture broader psychodynamic patterns. The historical persistence of the theory of the epileptic personality reflects the influence of constitutional frameworks and their potential for stigmatization, offering enduring insights into the complex interplay among psychiatry, psychology, psychometrics, and medical and social perceptions. (PsycInfo Database Record (c) 2026 APA, all rights reserved).
Proton-pump inhibitor-induced severe hypomagnesemia presenting with seizure and functional hypoparathyroidism: A diagnostic challenge in long-term acid suppression therapy.推荐
PMID 42969997 · Indian journal of pharmacology · 2026 11 02
作者：Hassan Y, Sarjanya G, Duraiswamy J, Ram P
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Proton-pump inhibitors (PPIs) are popular drugs used to treat acid-related disorders. The PPIs, though effective and relatively safe, have been linked to clinically significant hypomagnesemia with long-term use, which causes potentially life-threatening complications. A 68-year-old male with an 8-year history of omeprazole 40 mg/day use presented with a generalized tonic-clonic seizure and neuromuscular irritability. Laboratory tests showed severe hypomagnesemia (0.32 mmol/L), hypocalcemia (corrected calcium 1.62 mmol/L), hypokalaemia (3.2 mmol/L), and low parathyroid hormone (12 pg/mL). 24-h urine magnesium was low (12 mg/day), suggesting external magnesium loss. Serum Vitamin D levels and renal function tests were within normal limits. Electrocardiography showed Corrected QT (QTc) interval prolongation (510 ms) with ventricular ectopy. Magnesium levels failed to normalize despite intravenous replacement until omeprazole was discontinued and replaced with famotidine. PPI-induced hypomagnesemia should be suspected in patients on long-term therapy presenting with electrolyte disturbances, seizures, or arrhythmias. Recognition is crucial, as correction is often refractory until the offending drug is withdrawn.
MELAS Associated With a Large-Scale MT-ND2 Deletion: A Case Report.浏览
PMID 42969975 · The neurologist · 2026 11 02
作者：Wang W, Mao C, Yuan H, Zheng Q
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Mitochondrial encephalomyopathy, lactic acidosis, and stroke-like episodes (MELAS) is a clinically and genetically heterogeneous mitochondrial disorder. Although the m.3243A>G variant in MT-TL1 is the most common molecular cause, atypical cases with negative routine genetic testing and nondiagnostic muscle biopsy may pose a substantial diagnostic challenge. Here, we report a clinically diagnosed MELAS case with supportive neuropathological findings and a large-scale deletion involving MT-ND2. CASE REPORT: A 20-year-old man presented with recurrent seizures and encephalopathic episodes over 1.5 years. His clinical course was characterized by episodic visual disturbance, hearing impairment, hemiparesis, and progressive encephalopathic deterioration. Serial magnetic resonance imaging revealed recurrent migratory cortical lesions involving the occipital, temporal, and frontal lobes, and magnetic resonance spectroscopy demonstrated a lactate peak. Cerebrospinal fluid analysis and autoimmune and infectious investigations were unremarkable. Electromyography, muscle biopsy, and initial next-generation sequencing, including testing for the common m.3243A>G variant, were nondiagnostic. Subsequent brain biopsy demonstrated laminar cortical necrosis, supporting a mitochondrial encephalopathic process. Multiplex ligation-dependent probe amplification further identified a large-scale pathogenic deletion involving MT-ND2. Based on the characteristic clinical course, neuroimaging pattern, neuropathological findings, and complementary genetic analysis, a diagnosis of MELAS was established. CONCLUSION: This case expands the diagnostic spectrum of MELAS and highlights that typical clinical and radiologic features may still be present even when routine lactate testing, muscle biopsy, and standard genetic screening are nondiagnostic. In highly suspected cases, brain biopsy and complementary methods for mitochondrial structural variation analysis, such as MLPA, may provide crucial diagnostic evidence.
Treatment of status epilepticus: Too low, too slow.推荐
PMID 42969944 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 02
作者：Wasterlain CG, Rifkin RA, Suchomelova L, Williams J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：This review of the treatment of convulsive status epilepticus (SE) in adults examines why, with so many new tools, we have made so little progress. Major advances in basic mechanisms and increased number of available drugs, clinical trials, and publications have not led to a commensurate improvement in clinical outcomes. The central role of N-methyl-D-aspartate (NMDA) receptors in initiation and maintenance of SE is now understood, but its therapeutic implications have not been translated into clinical trials or clinical practice. Failure of inhibition from inactivation of synaptic γ-aminobutyric acid type A receptors (GABAARs) and KCC2 chloride transporters and runaway excitation from increases in NMDA receptors (NMDARs) and α-amino-3-hydroxy-5-methyl-4-isoxazolepropionic acid receptors (AMPARs) are all under NMDAR control, yet no current guidelines recommend early treatment with NMDAR antagonists. Time plays a critical role in SE-associated morbidity and mortality, and in the response to benzodiazepines and antiseizure medications, yet most patients are treated after time t2 and are often underdosed. Traditional SE treatment leaves too much time for NMDAR-dependent pharmacoresistance to develop. There is a gap between the therapeutic strategies suggested by preclinical studies and the design of clinical trials, and between therapeutic recommendations from clinical trials and their real-life application (too low, too slow). Prehospital treatment should be standardized, home treatment should be encouraged, and underdosing is not acceptable. "Time-to-needle" must become an integral part of SE treatment. In untreated SE, if treatment delay (stage "1 plus") predicts failure of benzodiazepine monotherapy, more vigorous initial treatment should be considered. A recent double-blind pediatric SE study showed that initial treatment with midazolam plus ketamine was superior to and better tolerated than midazolam alone; we need a similar clinical trial in adults. When benzodiazepines fail, time to the next agent should be minimized, and we need a prehospital trial of ketamine as second agent. We have the tools to improve the outcome of SE; let us use them.
Levetiracetam for pediatric convulsive status epilepticus in emergency settings: A systematic review and meta-analysis comparing levetiracetam, phenytoin, and valproate.推荐
PMID 42969890 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 02
作者：Chen X, Lin X, Wen Z
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Convulsive status epilepticus (CSE) is a life-threatening neurological emergency in children. When first-line benzodiazepines fail, the optimal second-line antiseizure medication remains debated. This systematic review and meta-analysis compared the efficacy and safety of intravenous levetiracetam (LEV) versus phenytoin/fosphenytoin (PHT/FOS) and versus valproate (VPA) as second-line treatments for pediatric CSE. We systematically searched PubMed, Embase, Cochrane Library, and Web of Science from inception to May 2026 for randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing LEV with PHT/FOS or VPA in children aged 3 months to 18 years with benzodiazepine-refractory CSE. The primary outcome was clinical cessation of CSE. Secondary outcomes included seizure recurrence within 24 h, need for intubation, and serious adverse events (SAEs). Pooled risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated using a random-effects model. Risk of bias was assessed using Cochrane RoB 2. Nine RCTs (1714 patients) were included. For LEV vs. PHT/FOS, no significant difference was found in seizure cessation (RR = 1.05, 95% CI .99 to 1.13; I2 = 38%; 9 studies, n = 1610). LEV showed a trend toward lower seizure recurrence within 24 h (RR = .77, 95% CI .55 to 1.09, p = .14; I2 = 0%; seven studies, n = 765). Need for intubation (RR = .69, 95% CI .40 to 1.21; I2 = 68%; six studies, n = 899) and SAEs (RR = 1.18, 95% CI .88 to 1.60; I2 = 0%; five studies, n = 834) did not differ significantly between groups. For LEV vs. VPA, no differences were observed in seizure cessation (RR = 1.05, 95% CI .87 to 1.27; I2 = 0%; two studies, n = 221), seizure recurrence (RR = 1.04, 95% CI .47 to 2.30; I2 = 0%; two studies, n = 213), intubation (RR = .69, 95% CI .32 to 1.51; I2 = 0%; two studies, n = 221), or SAEs (RR = .20, 95% CI .02 to 1.71; I2 = 0%; two studies, n = 221). No statistically significant differences in efficacy were found among levetiracetam, phenytoin/fosphenytoin, and valproate as second-line treatments for pediatric convulsive status epilepticus. Levetiracetam offers practical advantages in administration and safety, supporting its use as an appropriate first-choice second-line agent. Further well-designed comparative studies with standardized outcome assessments are needed.
Diagnostic value of extending outpatient EEG to record sleep - A retrospective study.推荐
PMID 42969794 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 02
作者：Dirks EHM, Amrutha PC, Pukkayil G, Villery A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Evidence for the utility of sleep electroencephalography (EEG) in the diagnosis of epilepsy is inconsistent, often necessitating repeat visits and sleep deprivation (SD), which increases patient and resource burden. In our setting in India, patients are often referred for EEG immediately following consultation (single visit) and EEG sessions are extended to allow for spontaneous sleep acquisition without prior SD. We aimed to evaluate the success rate of achieving sleep during these extended outpatient recordings and its incremental diagnostic yield for interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs). METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed 1762 outpatient EEGs from patients (45% female) with suspected or known epilepsy. All patients underwent an extended routine EEG (~40-50 min), incorporating standard activation procedures and aiming to capture spontaneous sleep where possible. Successful sleep acquisition was defined as the attainment of at least NREM stage N2. IED detection rates were compared between wakefulness and sleep across different epilepsy types. RESULTS: Sleep was achieved in 70.5% of patients (median age, 28 years; IQR: 16-55; range, 0.3-90). The proportion attaining sleep increased with recording duration, from 36.7% at 20 min to 55.6% at 30 min and 66.7% at 50 min, with a median time to sleep onset of 20 min. Among 1199 patients with both awake and sleep states captured, wakefulness identified IEDs in 29% (n = 350), while sleep captured unique IEDs in an additional 12% (n = 141), increasing the total yield significantly. In a sub-analysis of confirmed epilepsy cases (n = 751), wakefulness showed IEDs in 42% (n = 315), with sleep adding another 16% (n = 123). The diagnostic yield of sleep recordings was significantly higher than that of wakefulness alone (OR: 1.31; 95% CI: 1.02-1.68; p = 0.043), particularly among adults with idiopathic generalized epilepsy (IGE) (OR: 2.20; 95% CI: 1.30-3.73; p = 0.004) and children with self-limited epilepsy (OR: ∞; p = 0.031). SIGNIFICANCE: Despite a busy outpatient setting, 70.5% of patients attained N2 sleep during an extended EEG recording without SD. Sleep capture increased progressively with recording duration, with the greatest incremental gain between 20 and 30 min, suggesting that a modest extension beyond 20 min substantially increases the likelihood of capturing sleep. Sleep recording increased IED detection over wakefulness alone, particularly in children, in adults with IGE, and in self-limited epilepsies. Whether this approach can substitute for a sleep-deprived EEG requires prospective, head-to-head evaluation.
Performance of the Lausanne Ischemic Stroke Pathway prehospital triage system in identifying acute reperfusion candidates: a quality-control study.推荐
PMID 42969076 · Swiss medical weekly · 2026 10 29
作者：Bianchi C, Michel P, Strambo D, Dami F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVE: Accurate identification is one of the cornerstones of prehospital care for patients with suspected acute ischaemic stroke (AIS). In the Lausanne Ischemic Stroke Pathway triage system, emergency medical service (EMS) providers use dedicated criteria to identify acute reperfusion candidates and call a coordinated medical team (CMT, including a neurologist and an emergency physician), which can activate the stroke code pathway. The objective of this study was to evaluate the performance of the system in identifying acute reperfusion candidates. METHODS: This was a retrospective single-centre quality-control study conducted in a Swiss university-based stroke centre, from 1 January 2019 to 31 May 2022. Inclusion criteria were patients aged ≥18 years, for whom the EMS called the CMT or patients with AIS eligible for acute reperfusion managed by the EMS. The main outcomes were the performance of both the EMS and the CMT in detecting AIS patients eligible for acute reperfusion, as assessed through their respective under- and overtriage rates. A secondary outcome was the identification of alternative diagnoses in overtriaged patients. RESULTS: Of 1071 patients included, the EMS phone-contacted the CMT for 937 (87.5%) of them. Among the 1071 patients, 440 (41%) had an AIS eligible for reperfusion. The system's undertriage rate was 30% (133 patients without stroke pathway activation among the 440 patients with an AIS eligible for acute reperfusion). The undertriage rates were 21% for the EMS and 11% for the CMT. The overtriage rates were 63% for the EMS and 57% for the CMT. The main alternative diagnoses in overtriaged patients were seizures (14%), intracranial haemorrhages (9.9%) and functional disorders (6.5%). CONCLUSION: Undertriage and overtriage rates were relatively high, but both were reduced by the CMT call, highlighting the added value of a second expert assessment step, with a higher level of expertise. Concerning overtriage, a better prehospital identification of alternative diagnoses, such as seizures or functional disorders, could help to better classify our patients.
The Many Faces of Familial Mediterranean Fever in Children: Lessons From a Moroccan Cohort and Four Diagnostic Pitfalls.浏览
PMID 42968970 · Cureus · 2026 10 02
作者：Mohamed El Kerim M, Sara A, Elhaddadi H, Elouali A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Familial Mediterranean fever (FMF) is the prototypical hereditary autoinflammatory disease. Inherited in an autosomal recessive pattern, it is associated with mutations in the MEditerranean FeVer (MEFV) gene. Its clinical heterogeneity poses a major diagnostic challenge in pediatrics, particularly in developing countries where access to genetic testing remains limited. OBJECTIVES: To describe the clinical, epidemiological, biological, and genetic characteristics of pediatric FMF in the Oriental region of Morocco and to document diagnostic pitfalls through illustrative case reports. MATERIALS AND METHODS: A retrospective descriptive study including 14 children followed for FMF in the Pediatrics Department of the Mohammed VI University Hospital in Oujda over an 11.5-year period (January 2015 to June 2026). The diagnosis was based on the Yalçınkaya-Özen pediatric criteria and/or genetic confirmation via MEFV gene sequencing. RESULTS: The mean age at diagnosis was 6.5 years (range: 2-14 years). A marked male predominance was observed in 12 (85.7%); sex ratio (6:1). Parental consanguinity was present in 5/14 (35.7%) of cases. All patients were from the Oriental region 14/14 (100.0%). The predominant clinical manifestations were recurrent fever in 14 (100.0%), abdominal pain in 12/14 (85.7%), and arthralgia/arthritis in 11/14 (78.6%). MEFV gene sequencing was positive in 9/14 (64.3%) of patients, with a predominance of the M694V mutation in 5/14 (35.7%), of whom 3/5 (60.0%) were homozygous. Four illustrative case reports document atypical presentations that resulted in a diagnostic delay of more than two years: a polyarticular form mimicking juvenile idiopathic arthritis (JIA), a coronary aneurysm mistaken for Kawasaki disease, recurrent febrile seizures, and predominantly erysipelas-like erythema. Colchicine was effective in all 14/14 (100.0%) treated cases. No amyloid amyloidosis (AA) was detected during follow-up. CONCLUSION: Pediatric FMF is underdiagnosed in the Oriental region of Morocco. Its clinical polymorphism leads to diagnostic delays with harmful consequences. A rigorous clinical evaluation supported by targeted genetic sequencing is essential for early diagnosis and prompt initiation of colchicine therapy.
Severe Propofol Infusion Syndrome Managed With Multimodal Critical Care: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 42968967 · Cureus · 2026 10 31
作者：Power R, El Katan M, Wibrow B, Anstey MH
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Rapid cardiovascular collapse with severe metabolic derangement during propofol sedation should raise concern for propofol infusion syndrome (PRIS). A 21-year-old man with previous traumatic brain injury presented with refractory status epilepticus requiring mechanical ventilation and prolonged propofol sedation. Following four days of infusion, he developed abrupt bradycardia, cardiogenic shock, severe high-anion-gap metabolic acidosis, rhabdomyolysis, acute kidney injury, hypertriglyceridemia, and significant left ventricular dysfunction. The combination of prolonged propofol exposure and rapidly evolving multiorgan failure was considered consistent with PRIS. Propofol was discontinued immediately. Management included administration of multiple vasoactive infusions, sodium bicarbonate, calcium chloride, dual high-effluent continuous renal replacement therapy via two dialysis circuits, high-dose euglycemic insulin, and enteral L-carnitine. Hemodynamic improvement occurred within approximately six hours, followed by recovery of cardiac and renal function. He was discharged from the intensive care unit on day 30 and later returned home after rehabilitation without dialysis dependence. This case highlights the importance of early recognition of PRIS and rapid escalation of individualized organ support in severe presentations, while acknowledging that the contribution of individual adjunctive therapies cannot be determined from a single case.
Explainable Machine Learning for Predicting Early Response to Perampanel in Pediatric Epilepsy: A Real-World Study.推荐
PMID 42968942 · Drug design, development and therapy · 2026 10 28
作者：Rao X, Wu P, Lin X, Zhang JM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To develop and validate an early prediction model for perampanel (PER) response in children with epilepsy (CWE) using clinical covariates and ensemble learning, to support individualized treatment decisions. METHODS: A retrospective cohort of 325 CWE (< 15 years) treated with PER from January 2021 to December 2025 was enrolled. Treatment response at 2 months was defined as the study outcome (responder vs non-responder). Key covariates were screened using Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LGBM) feature importance ranking combined with forward feature selection. Five core algorithms were selected from 11 candidate algorithms, and a soft voting ensemble model was constructed, interpreted using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), and subjected to age-stratified analysis. RESULTS: Seven covariates were ultimately included. The ensemble model achieved a test set AUC of 0.861 (95% CI: 0.727-0.963), accuracy of 0.892 (95% CI: 0.815-0.954), and specificity of 0.940 (95% CI: 0.860-1.000); the mean 5-fold cross-validation AUC was 0.915 ± 0.064; external validation yielded an AUC of 0.798 (95% CI: 0.668-0.900). Decision curve analysis (DCA) demonstrated improved net benefit within threshold probabilities of 0.07 to 0.75. Calibration curves showed good calibration (brier score = 0.12). SHAP analysis identified the number of antiseizure medications (ASMs, 43.7%), PER maintenance dose (17.0%), and CYP3A4 enzyme inducers (CYP3A4-I, 13.1%) as the primary predictors, all positively associated with treatment non-response. Exploratory age-stratified SHAP analysis (≤72 months vs >72 months) suggested that the associations of polytherapy, higher PER maintenance dose, and shorter disease duration with predicted treatment non-response may be more pronounced in younger children. CONCLUSION: In this pediatric cohort, the ensemble model demonstrated good discrimination and calibration for predicting early perampanel response at 2 months. The number of ASMs, PER maintenance dose, and CYP3A4-I were the leading predictors of treatment non-response.
Atypical Pediatric Epilepsy: A Narrative Review of Classification, Diagnosis, and Emerging Therapeutics.推荐
PMID 42968794 · Cureus · 2026 10 02
作者：Iglesias M, Sherin K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Atypical pediatric epilepsies represent a heterogeneous group of disorders characterized by uncommon etiologies, variable clinical presentations, and frequent resistance to standard antiseizure medications. Recent advances in classification, along with developments in genomics, neuroimaging, and autoimmune neurology, have expanded the recognized spectrum of these conditions. However, a significant proportion of children remain without a definitive syndromic diagnosis, underscoring ongoing limitations in current frameworks. This review provides a comprehensive overview of atypical epilepsy in children, including evolving classification systems, epidemiology, and etiological mechanisms spanning genetic, structural, metabolic, and immune-mediated causes. Key syndromes - including Lennox-Gastaut syndrome, Dravet syndrome, infantile epileptic spasms syndrome, and febrile infection-related epilepsy syndrome - are discussed with emphasis on clinical features, differential diagnosis, and prognosis. Diagnostic challenges such as phenotypic overlap, EEG limitations, and barriers to timely genetic testing are also examined. Emerging therapeutic strategies highlight a paradigm shift from broad-spectrum seizure control toward precision medicine, including targeted pharmacologic agents, antisense oligonucleotides, gene therapies, and neuromodulation approaches. Improved recognition and classification of atypical epilepsies, coupled with early implementation of targeted therapies, are essential to optimize outcomes in this vulnerable population.
Clinical features and long-term outcomes of anti-DPPX autoimmune encephalitis: a multicentre retrospective study in a Chinese cohort.推荐
PMID 42968772 · BMJ neurology open · 2026 10 29
作者：Chen F, Yu W, Peng J, Xue T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To explore the clinical characteristics and long-term prognostic factors of anti-Dipeptidyl-peptidase-like protein 6 (DPPX) antibody encephalitis in Chinese patients. METHOD: This study retrospectively included anti-DPPX antibody encephalitis patients diagnosed at three medical centres from January 2019 to May 2024. modified Rankin Scale (mRS) and Clinical Assessment Scale in Autoimmune Encephalitis (CASE) were used to evaluate baseline, short-term (3 months) and long-term (12 months) outcomes. Furthermore, we analysed the relationship between clinical characteristics, immune markers and prognosis. RESULTS: 15 patients were included (median age 45, 60% female). Predominant symptoms were cognitive impairment (66.7%, 10/15), seizures (53.3%, 8/15) and psychiatric symptoms (53.3%, 8/15), with a low incidence of preceding weight loss (6.7%, 1/15); 60.0% (9/15) of patients had abnormal brain MRI findings. Short-term disease severity (3M-CASE) was significantly positively correlated with hospital stay (β=0.22, 95% CI 0.02 to 0.42, p=0.03), psychiatric disorders (β=2.71, 95% CI 0.26 to 5.17, p=0.03), intensive care unit admission (β=4.11, 95% CI 2.07 to 6.15, p=0.001) and MRI abnormalities (β=3.13, 95% CI 0.73 to 5.52, p=0.02). Long-term disease severity (12M-CASE) was significantly correlated with sleep disorders (β=2.72, 95% CI 0.36 to 5.08, p=0.03). No specific immunological biomarkers remained significantly correlated with CASE scores after adjustment for multiple comparisons. No clinical relapse was observed in the 11 patients with available long-term follow-up data. CONCLUSION: This study delineates the clinical phenotype of anti-DPPX encephalitis in China. Immunotherapy was significantly associated with improvements in short-term and long-term outcomes. Prognostic factors and relevant immune biomarkers differ between disease stages, underscoring the need for long-term management and stage-specific immunointervention.
Consensus recommendations on diagnosis and management of posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome in paediatric haematopoietic stem cell transplantation.推荐
PMID 42968593 · British journal of haematology · 2026 11 01
作者：Leardini D, Bergonzini L, Muratore E, Pace V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is a rare but potentially life-threatening neurological complication following paediatric allogeneic haematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT). Its heterogeneous presentation and the absence of standardized guidance contribute to variability in diagnosis and management. The aim was to develop multidisciplinary, consensus-based recommendations for the diagnosis, management and follow-up of PRES in paediatric allogeneic HSCT recipients. A modified Delphi process was conducted between February and September 2025. An international panel comprising transplant physicians, neurologists, neuro-oncologists and neuroradiologists participated in four iterative rounds. A systematic literature review informed statement development across five domains: pathogenesis, clinical features, diagnostics, management and follow-up. Predefined agreement thresholds determined consensus. Thirty-eight statements achieved consensus in the first round, indicating strong agreement. Key recommendations addressed risk factors, early clinical recognition, appropriate neuroimaging and management of contributory factors including hypertension and immunosuppressive therapy. Emphasis was placed on multidisciplinary care, prompt diagnosis and tailored management. Guidance on neurological monitoring and long-term follow-up was also established. These consensus recommendations provide a standardized framework for the management of PRES in paediatric allogeneic HSCT recipients. Their implementation may improve early recognition, optimize treatment and enhance outcomes, while highlighting priorities for future research and prospective guideline development.
Long-Term Drop-Seizure Response Patterns to Cannabidiol According to Genetic Status in Lennox-Gastaut Syndrome.推荐
PMID 42968542 · CNS neuroscience & therapeutics · 2026 Nov
作者：Na JH, Cho HY, Jeon H, Lee H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Lennox-Gastaut syndrome (LGS) is a genetically heterogeneous developmental and epileptic encephalopathy in which variability in long-term cannabidiol (CBD) response remains incompletely understood. We evaluated whether long-term drop-seizure response trajectories to CBD differ according to genetic status. METHODS: This single-center retrospective longitudinal study analyzed systematically collected clinical follow-up data from 61 patients with LGS treated with pharmaceutical-grade CBD; 46 patients with ≥ 24 months of follow-up were included. Patients were classified as pathogenic/likely pathogenic variant-positive (PV+) or variant-negative (PV-). Drop-seizure reduction trajectories were assessed at 3-month intervals over 24 months. RESULTS: Drop-seizure reduction improved rapidly and was sustained over 24 months in both PV+ and PV- groups, with no significant differences between groups at the prespecified comparison time points. Longitudinal response trajectories showed substantial overlap, and sustained response was observed in 76.1% of patients. CONCLUSION: Long-term drop-seizure response to CBD in LGS was sustained but not stratified by genetic status, supporting a syndrome-level treatment approach irrespective of genetic findings.
Expression of Tachykinin 2/Neurokinin B and Neuropeptide Y and the Functional Contribution of Neuropeptide Y in the Mouse Dentate Gyrus following Repeated Electroconvulsive Seizure.推荐
PMID 42968257 · Biological & pharmaceutical bulletin · 2026
作者：Kashiwabara R, Suzuki H, Waguri I, Akimoto N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Electroconvulsive therapy is an effective treatment option for major depression. Studies using rodent electroconvulsive seizure (ECS) models have suggested that the dentate gyrus (DG) of the hippocampal formation is critically involved and that ECS induces robust transcriptomic changes in the DG. Although previous studies have suggested that the expression of two neuropeptide genes, Tachykinin 2 (TAC2) and neuropeptide Y (Npy), is induced by repeated ECS in the DG, the temporal and spatial expression patterns of these genes and their functional contribution to the DG remain unknown. In this study, we found that Tac2 mRNA expression was induced by repeated ECS in the subgranular zone (SGZ), a neurogenic niche, of the DG. In contrast, the expression of Npy mRNA was induced by single and repeated ECS in the hilus. Immunohistochemical analysis revealed that repeated ECS increased the number of NPY-positive cells exhibiting low or undetectable somatostatin expression in the hilus, suggesting the presence of a stimulus-responsive cell population. We further found that the ECS-induced increase in immature neurons in the SGZ was partially suppressed by intracerebroventricular infusion of the NPY-Y1 receptor antagonist, suggesting a modulatory role for NPY-Y1 signaling in ECS-induced neurogenesis. These findings suggest that ECS engages neuropeptide-mediated neuromodulation in a distinct temporal and spatial pattern in the DG.
Intraoperative indocyanine green fluorescence in canine meningioma: macroscopic visualization, immediate MRI correlation, and clinical outcomes : a case series.推荐
PMID 42968180 · BMC veterinary research · 2026 08 03
作者：Kim Y, Choi M, Kim Y, Kim HY
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Meningioma is the most common primary intracranial neoplasm in dogs, frequently presenting with seizures and focal neurological deficits. While indocyanine green (ICG) near-infrared (NIR) fluorescence is increasingly used in human neurosurgery, systematic data regarding the macroscopic fluorescence emitted by accumulated ICG in canine meningiomas remain scarce. This case series aimed to evaluate the fluorescent signals emitted from the tumors, correlate intraoperative findings using immediate post-operative contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and report the clinical outcomes and post-operative management following fluorescence-guided surgery (FGS). CASE PRESENTATION: Six client-owned dogs (median age: 13 years) with histologically confirmed meningiomas were evaluated. ICG was administered intravenously (2.5-5.0 mg/kg, median dose: 4.5 mg/kg) 6 to 24 h prior to surgery. FGS was performed using an ultrasonic aspirator and a dedicated fluorescence imaging system. Distinct margin fluorescence was observed in all 6 cases (100%), allowing for real-time differentiation between tumor tissue and grossly normal brain parenchyma during resection. Immediate post-operative T1-weighted contrast-enhanced MRI demonstrated 100% concordance with intraoperative wound bed fluorescence assessments when thin reactive rims (≤ 1-2 mm) were appropriately excluded. No adverse reactions were associated with ICG. The median follow-up was 14 months (range: 6.6 to 31.0 months), with favorable neurological control. CONCLUSIONS: Utilizing a second-window approach with dynamic working distance control, ICG effectively demarcated macroscopic canine meningiomas in this small cohort. Immediate post-operative MRI correlated well with surgeon's intraoperative assessments. Ultimately, FGS may serve as a viable surgical option to help minimize damage to grossly normal brain tissue and potentially improve surgical outcomes in canine meningioma.
Successful surgical retrieval of an intracranial sewing needle retained for more than 45 years since infancy with complete fibrous encapsulation: a case report and literature review.推荐
PMID 42968086 · BMC surgery · 2026 08 05
作者：Xiong T, Ma JC, Liu WX, Sun H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Intracranial sewing needles are an extremely rare neurosurgical condition. Such injuries mostly occur during infancy, with the sewing needle penetrating intracranially through unclosed fontanelles. The clinical manifestations of intracranially retained sewing needles are often insidious and nonspecific. The sewing needle often remains in the brain for prolonged periods until it is discovered in adulthood due to an accidental imaging examination or the appearance of symptoms such as headache and epileptic seizures. CASE PRESENTATION: In this report, an exceptionally rare case of a 47-year-old woman with a sewing needle retained intracranially for more than 45 years since infancy is described. The sole clinical manifestation in recent years was recurrent dizziness. The needle was likely inserted accidentally through the anterior fontanelle during infancy. We performed a microsurgical operation. During the operation, the sewing needle was observed to be completely encapsulated by organized fibrous tissue, forming a fibrous sleeve-like structure that enclosed it. The rusty sewing needle was successfully removed, and part of the encapsulating cyst wall was resected. The patient's dizziness disappeared after surgery, and no new neurological symptoms were observed during the six-month follow-up period. CONCLUSIONS: The characteristic presentation of long-term intracranially retained sewing needles may be an organized fibrous capsule. In this specific case, adopting a surgical strategy aimed at removing the sewing needle and debriding the cyst cavity-rather than performing aggressive total resection of the fibrous capsule-is a reasonable approach that balances foreign-body removal with brain protection.
Efficacy and safety of combined intraventricular/intrathecal and intravenous polymyxin B for postsurgical neurosurgical infections due to MDR/XDR-GNB: a retrospective cohort study.推荐
PMID 42968049 · BMC neurology · 2026 07 28
作者：Wan DY, Liang MW, Luo XY, Bai X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Intracranial infections caused by multidrug-resistant or extensively drug-resistant Gram-negative bacteria (MDR/XDR-GNB) represent a significant challenge in neurosurgical critical care, with limited effective treatment options. This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of intraventricular/intrathecal (IVT/ITH) and intravenous polymyxin B in patients with post-neurosurgical MDR/XDR-GNB intracranial infections, focusing on long-term outcomes and neurotoxicity. METHODS: This multicenter retrospective cohort study was conducted across seven hospitals in the West China region between December 2019 and June 2021. Patients with culture-confirmed MDR/XDR-GNB intracranial infection were enrolled and divided into two groups based on whether they received IVT/ITH polymyxin B. The primary outcome was 90-day mortality, with secondary outcomes including pathogen clearance rate, neurological status at discharge, and adverse events. These findings support its use as a promising therapeutic strategy in resource-limited settings where this combined local-systemic regimen is not feasible. TRAIL REGISTRATION: This study was approved by the Ethics Committee of West China Hospital, approval number 278.

Adjunctive perampanel in children with focal-onset seizures and developmental delay or intellectual disability: efficacy, tolerability, and sleep benefits.推荐
PMID 42967411 · Brain & development · 2026 11 01
作者：Kang YM, Kim YO
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Perampanel (PER), a noncompetitive alpha-amino-3-hydroxy-5-methyl-4-isoxazolepropionic acid receptor antagonist, is used to treat focal-onset seizures in patients aged ≥4 years. However, evidence in children with focal-onset seizures and developmental delay (DD) and/or intellectual disability (ID) remains limited, particularly regarding sleep-related outcomes. This study evaluated the efficacy and tolerability of adjunctive PER in this population. METHODS: This retrospective single-cohort study assessed the effectiveness of adjunctive PER for overall and sleep-related seizures and sleep disturbance, as well as tolerability based on adverse events and treatment retention, in patients aged <18 years with focal-onset seizures and DD/ID treated from January 2020 through December 2025. Among 29 patients with preexisting sleep disturbance, 93.1% improved. Involvement of the Claustrum in Human Epileptic Seizures.推荐
PMID 42967170 · Iranian journal of child neurology · 2026 09 01
作者：Andrade Machado R, Meylor J

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: We aimed to assess if the claustrum is involved in seizure networks in humans with epilepsy. MATERIALS AND METHODS: We identified eight patients submitted for presurgical evaluation with stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG) recordings from the claustrum from 2016-2022. Demographic, paraclinical, and outcome data (Engel Classification) was collected from retrospective chart review. We analyzed the ictal and interictal pattern as well as high frequency oscillations (HFOs) in the claustrum. RESULTS: The ictal onset zone was in the temporal lobe in 87% (n=7) of patients and the cingulate cortex in 12% (n=1). A total of 25 seizures were detected and 96% (n=24) involved the claustrum cortex. Of those seizures involving the claustrum, 88% (n=21) were involved before the rest of the electrodes. Two ictal onset patterns were identified: slow potential shift with HFOs were found in 46% (n=11) of seizures and rhythmic spikes <13 hz were found in 54% of seizures (n=13). The ictal pattern in the claustrum was synchronous with the ictal onset zone in 41% (n=10) seizures, however, in four patients with status epilepticus, the claustrum was noted to stop discharging just before ictal onset. The interictal pattern included high-amplitude slow waves with or without HFOs. HFOs were identified in the claustrum in 83% (n=20) of post-ictal periods. All patients had poor outcomes following surgical resection (Engel Class III-IV). CONCLUSIONS: Our study suggests that the claustrum is often involved during seizures arising from the temporal lobe and cingulate cortexes and could play a role in modulation of the epileptogenic network.
Clinical History and Burden of Gaucher Disease Type 3: A Cross-Sectional Multinational Study.浏览
PMID 42966938 · JIMD reports · 2026 10 30
作者：Mengel E, Stanescu S, Di Fonzo A, Donald A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Gaucher disease Type 3 (GD3) is a rare genetic neurodegenerative disorder, characterized by heterogeneity in symptom burden, disease severity, and progression. There is limited evidence on the burden of GD3. This retrospective, cross-sectional study estimated the burden of illness associated with GD3 using descriptive analyses of clinician- and patient/caregiver-reported data from Germany, Italy, Spain, Turkey, the UK, and the US. A total of 79 patients were enrolled in the study; 39 patients and 38 caregivers completed the patient/caregiver survey. The majority of patients were male (74.7%) and under 18 years old (55.7%), with an average age at index (study enrolment date) of 17.0 years (SD = 9.0). Patients received a GD3 diagnosis at a mean age of 8.2 years (SD = 6.4). The most common first non-neurological symptoms experienced were splenomegaly (49.4%), anemia (44.3%), and enlarged abdomen (43.0%), while the first most common neurological manifestations reported included eye movement disorders (54.4%), epilepsy (35.4%), and neurodevelopmental delay (34.2%). Approximately half of patients experienced falls (55.7%) or difficulty with personal care (53.2%). Nearly half of patients (45.6%) had at least one hospitalization, with a mean (SD) of 2.1 (1.7) hospitalizations per patient, in the 2 years pre-index. Caregivers were predominantly female (86.8%) and provided an average of 63.3 h of care per week (SD = 40.2). They reported high work impairment (70.3%, SD = 22.0; WPAI) and reduced quality of life (CarerQoL-7D mean = 67.0, SD = 15.8). This study characterizes the real-world burden of GD3, providing an up-to-date, multinational perspective on its clinical history and impact on patients, caregivers, healthcare systems, and society.
Developmental and genetic modulation of evidence integration dynamics in zebrafish sensorimotor decision-making.浏览
PMID 42966776 · Communications biology · 2026 10 29
作者：Garza R, El Hady A, Bahl A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
关联文献（UpdateOf）：PMID 41847006
摘要：Animals integrate information over time and maintain persistent internal representations of cues to guide decision-making. How the underlying behavioral algorithms of individual animals depend on factors such as experience, developmental stage, or genotype remains poorly understood. Drift-diffusion models provide a powerful theoretical framework to describe and predict performance metrics across a wide range of species. The stochastic nature of these models and the typically limited throughput of most experimental designs challenge the automatic inference of latent variables. Here, we combine high-throughput behavioral assays in larval zebrafish with drift-diffusion modeling, revealing that larvae progressively develop more persistent, self-reinforcing integration dynamics during early development. This effect is reduced in fish carrying mutations in genes linked to human epilepsy and schizophrenia. Our results show that behavior-based drift-diffusion modeling can offer a scalable, automated approach to generate experimentally testable hypotheses about the algorithmic implementation of sensorimotor integration in health and disease.
